> Cikakken bayani ga kowane canjin yanayi wanda OmniRoute ya gane shi. Don samfurin farawa mai sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kowane maɓalli da aka rubuta anan kuma dole ne ya bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, kuma kowane m a cikin `.env.example` dole ne ya bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync` yana tilasta wannan akan aikata kuma a cikin CI. Don ƙetare m da gangan, ƙara shi zuwa lissafin izini a cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Sirran da Ake Buƙata

Sirran da ba a saita ba waɗanda aka yi wa alamar **Eh** ana ƙirƙirar su ta atomatik a farkon aiki (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) sannan a adana su a `server.env` cikin kundin bayanai — saita su kai tsaye idan wannan kundin na wucin gadi ne. Banda wannan shi ne `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ba a adana shi a `server.env` — wuraren shigar uwar garken suna ƙirƙirar ƙimar wucin gadi ta kowace kunnawa wadda gada da ke cikin tsari kawai za ta iya amfani da ita, saboda haka abokan cinikin gada na waje suna buƙatar a saita ta kai tsaye.

| Sauyi                        | Wajibi           | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------- | ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Eh**           | _(babu)_                      | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Yana sanya hannu/tabbatar da duk kukis na zaman dashboard (JWT). Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Eh**           | _(ana ƙirƙira ta atomatik)_   | `src/shared/utils/apiKey.ts`                              | Sirri da ake amfani da shi wajen ƙirƙira da tabbatar da checksum na maɓallan API da aka ɗaure da na'ura. Idan ba a saita shi ba, tsarin farawa na yau da kullum yana samar da shi kuma yana adana shi a `server.env` idan ana iya rubuta wa fayil ɗin. Saita shi kai tsaye idan kundin bayanai na wucin gadi ne. Ƙirƙire shi da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Yana saita kalmar sirrin dashboard lokacin farawa muddin ba a riga an adana wata kalmar sirri ba; ana yin watsi da shi bayan an adana kalmar sirri. Shigarwar npm da ta lambar tushe suna kwafe `.env.example` zuwa `.env`, don haka suna farawa da sanannen ƙimar riƙon wuri `CHANGEME`: maye gurbinta kafin kunnawa ta farko, ko saita `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (fanko) a cikin wannan `.env` domin ƙirƙirar kalmar sirri a mayen fara amfani a ziyarar farko, kamar yadda ƙimar da ba a saita ba (tsohuwar ƙimar hoton Docker) take yi. Sauya kalmar sirri da aka adana a Dashboard → Settings → Security, ko gudanar da `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` a cikin kwafin lambar tushe) tare da saita `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin bayanan uwar garken. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Eh** (samarwa) | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin da aka raba don gadar WebSocket ta Codex Responses ta ciki. Yana tantance buƙatun gada tsakanin mai isar da WS na Electron/mai lilo da OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ANA BUƘATARSA a samarwa — idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ƙin duk buƙatun gadar WS.** Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a              | _(SHA na git)_                | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Takamaiman id na karya cache na service-worker don harsashin PWA (#11779); shi ne na farko a jerin tantancewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a jerin — masu gina PaaS (misali irin na Heroku) ne ke saita shi a matsayin commit ɗin da aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a              | _(an samo)_                   | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a ta lokacin ginawa da abokin ciniki ke amfani da ita don yin rajistar `/sw.js?v=…`; ana samo ta daga biyun da ke sama, sannan SHA na git.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (atomatik)   | _(atomatik a kowace kunnawa)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirrin kowane tsari da ke tabbatar da cewa tambarin IP na amintaccen takwara ya fito daga uwar garken HTTP ta OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware na authz yana amincewa da kasancewar buƙata a gida (takaita hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY zuwa loopback/LAN) ne kawai idan tambarin yana ɗauke da wannan token. Ana ƙirƙirarsa ta atomatik a kowace kunnawa — bar shi ba a saita ba; saita shi tabbatacce kawai don tsarin matakai da yawa waɗanda dole ne su raba tambarin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | Jerin IP ko kewayon CIDR na reverse proxies da aka raba da waƙafi, waɗanda jerin yarda/ƙin IP zai iya amincewa da `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` nasu, ban da loopback, adireshin hanyar sadarwa mai zaman kanta da gefunan Cloudflare, waɗanda ake amincewa da su koyaushe. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don proxy da ke kan wani adireshin jama'a dabam; in ba haka ba, adireshin proxy ɗin kansa ne matatar za ta tantance. Takaita yawan buƙatu na kowane abokin ciniki na relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) yana amfani da wannan adireshin abokin ciniki da aka tantance, ba kan taken turawa kaɗai ba.                                                                                                                                                         |

### Umarnin Ƙirƙirawa

```bash
# Ƙirƙiri dukkan sirran huɗu a lokaci guda:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kada a taɓa commit na fayilolin `.env` masu sirri na gaske zuwa tsarin sarrafa sigogi. `.gitignore` ya riga ya ware `.env`, amma tabbatar kafin turawa.

---

## 2. Adana & Database

OmniRoute yana amfani**SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) domin duk dagewa. Waɗannan masu canji suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da zagayen rayuwa.

| Mai canzawa                                | Tsohuwar                      | Fayil na asali                                        | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tushen directory don SQLite DB, madadin, da fayilolin bayanai. Shake don kundin Docker ko hanyoyin da aka saba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Tserewa ƙyanƙyashe don mai gadin test/eval DATA_DIR (#10428). Gwaje-gwaje da Node eval/print bincike (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gami da siffofin `--eval=`/`--print=` ) ba tare da `DATA_DIR` ana tura su zuwa madaidaicin jifa ba don haka ba za su iya buɗe ainihin bayanan mai aiki ba; saita zuwa `1` don komawa zuwa ainihin kundin adireshi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na kayan aikin gudu. An buga tambarin `npm run build:release`; allura a cikin kwantena waɗanda ke jigilar su ba tare da sakon `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Fitacce kamar `system.buildSha` akan `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref ƙofofin fakitin kayan tarihi yana bincika ginin SHA da (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita zuwa `1` don ba da damar shirya ginin wanda SHA baya kan layin sakin, yin rikodin shi azaman canary da gangan maimakon gazawar ƙofar (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | maɓallin API don binciken binciken hayaki na canary, wanda aka aika azaman `Authorization: Bearer` akan `/v1/chat/completions`. Rubutun turawa kawai ke amfani dashi (#10429), ba ta uwar garken ba. Ba ya da alaƙa da masu canjin `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na zaɓin shiga CLI kayan hayaki (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — duba [CLI Haɗin kai → Tsararriyar hayaki na gaske](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Siginar Gina-lokaci (#10060): saita zuwa `1` ta hanyar `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` kuma kowane ma'aikacin gini ya gaji don haka layin DB ya dawo da stub mara-op maimakon loda na asalin better-sqlite3 addon (wanda ke zubar da ma'aikaci yayin fita). Kar a taɓa saita uwar garken da ke gudana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake bincike na zaɓi na zaɓi na ɗan ƙasa na zaɓi don bishiyar da aka sayar. Wannan baya sanya abin dogaro da ya ɓace ya zama mai ginawa; amfani kawai lokacin da aka ba da abubuwan dogaro na asali ba tare da bandeji ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Fallback mai suna** don `DATA_DIR`, duba kawai lokacin da `DATA_DIR` bai saita ba. An yi amfani da shi don nemo ma'ajiyar ma'ajiyar zare mai mannewa akan faifan mai aiwatar da PromptQL (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan ba a saita var ba, cache ɗin yana tsayawa a cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya kawai (ba a ci gaba da farawa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Directory da** Runtime plugin scanner** yana karanta — da tushen mai sarrafa plugin ɗin yana sanyawa cikin — yana tsallake tsohowar gida da aka samu (#11827). Nuna shi a bishiyar plugin ɗin da aka ɗora a cikin Docker/K8s maimakon motsa HOME kawai don ƙaura hanyar binciken (HOME yana jagorantar kowane ɗabi'ar dangi ma). Cire = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` lokacin da tsarin ke fitar da gida gabaɗaya — rashin gano shiru wannan canjin yana cirewa. Ana shigar da adireshin da aka warware sau ɗaya a farawa azaman `scanner.dir_resolved` tare da shigarwar da ta ci nasara. Gefen uwar garken kawai: plugins umarni na CLI suna kiyaye nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe na 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(Ba a kashe = ba)_           | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | maɓallin AES don cikakken ɓoye bayanan SQLite a sauran. Ƙirƙiri tare da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar don maɓallin ɓoyewa. Haɓakawa lokacin yin jujjuyawar maɓalli don tallafawa ɓarna tsoffin ma'ajin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Lokacin da `true`, ya tsallake routine/pre-write SQLite fayil madadin (models.dev farashin save/clear, saituna ya rubuta). Manual da pre-dawowa madadin har yanzu suna aiki. Yana yi** ba** musaki hoton ƙaura mai dorewa mai ɗorewa ko mai gadin ƙaura don DB mai dorewa. Ajiye bayanan da ba na hannu ba ana matsawa zuwa aƙalla sau ɗaya a cikin mintuna 60. Dashboard** Saituna → Ma'aji** zai iya kashe madadin atomatik na yau da kullun da kansa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(unset/off)_                 | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Milliseconds; dabi'u da ke ƙasa 1 ko waɗanda ba na ƙididdigewa ba sun tsaya a kashe; yayi kashedin sau ɗaya a kowace 10s; layin log yana da direba, aiki, da elapsedMs kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_OPTIMIZE_ENABLED`        | `true`                        | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Ƙididdiga masu tsara bayanan baya, mai zaman kansa daga tsaftacewa mai riƙewa: izinin sharewa na farko da aka kunna (yawanci 30s bayan farawa, bayan ƙaura) yana gudanar da `PRAGMA optimize=0x10002`; wucewa na gaba kowane 6h gudu `PRAGMA optimize` (ko `PRAGMA optimize=0x00002` don bincike mara iyaka). `false` ko `0` yana hana duk wani kulawa (wanda ba shi da hankali, an gyara farar fata). Ma'ajin bayanai na ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya da hanyoyin ajiyar karatu kawai ba su da tasiri. An shigar da gazawar, ba a jefa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_ANALYSIS_LIMIT`          | `400`                         | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Saita `PRAGMA analysis_limit` kafin ingantawa. An manne adadin adadin goma zuwa [0, 100000]; ƙimar malformed/empty tsoho zuwa 400. `0` yana buƙatar mara iyaka, ainihin bincike na tebur da aka zaɓa ta hanyar ingantawa; izinin wucewa na lokaci-lokaci yana share SQLite's inganta samfurin-cap bit don girmama wannan. Ba a gudanar da bayanin bincike SQL na tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy alias** za `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Karɓi azaman koma baya lokacin da farkon farkon ba ya nan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy alias** ( sifar Base64-encoded ) karɓuwa azaman koma baya. Ƙaddamarwa ta atomatik kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Madaidaicin ba shi da aiki: ingantacciyar ƙima tana yin rajistar gargaɗin yanke hukunci na lokaci ɗaya, yayin da `0` ko rashin saitawa ke yin shiru. | `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Har yaushe matsatsi mai mahimmanci dole ne ya ci gaba kafin sake farawa da kai wuta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don yin watsi da `/proc/pressure/memory` mai fa'ida (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) lokacin da maƙwabci mai hayaniya yayi tafiya 503s yayin da wannan tsari yayi kyau (#13124). Cgroup da V8 masu gadin tulin suna tsayawa. PSI kuma yana dakatar da kirgawa azaman hujjar magudanar kwaya don `cgroup_high`: jimlar rukuni sama da `memory.high` sannan ta zama mai mahimmanci kawai akan karuwar `memory.events` `high`/`max`. Fayilolin da aka samo a wurin ana yin rikodin su azaman `<namespace>-<number>`, don haka rarraba jigilar jigilar nasa ba zai taɓa yin karo da ramummuka na sama ba. Shigar da ba ta da tsari, wurin suna mara inganci ko bayanan da ya ɓace yana jefawa a farawa maimakon yin shiru da tsallake tsarin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Unset yana rubuta `<path>.generated` kusa da fayil ɗin da aka ɗauka kawai. Yanayin mai watsa shiri kawai: ba a taɓa adana shi a cikin bayanan ba ko karɓa ta hanyar gudanarwa API, saboda ana aiwatar da binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

An kiyaye OFF ta tsohuwa don kada log ɗin tsari ya yoyo IPs ko prefix na asusun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` don buga binciken binciken lokaci na kowane-buƙata (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin keɓaɓɓen CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima mara amfani don fitar da binciken `[omniroute completion]` daga hanyoyin cache harsashi CLI (read/refresh/write). A kashe ta tsohuwa — waɗancan cache ɗin sun kasa yin shiru don haka cache ɗin missing/corrupt ba zai taɓa karyawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin sake gwadawa taga don daidaitattun abubuwa (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan lokacin an yi alama sun kasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkirin tushe (ms) don juzu'i na juzu'i akan sake fasalin abu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Tafi (ms) don juzu'i mai ma'ana tsakanin matakan sake gwadawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin batches da aka sarrafa lokaci guda. Tada don ƙara yawan kayan aiki; kiyaye ƙasa don guje wa guguwa mai iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin canza bayanan dagewar data kasance, mai gudun hijira yana buga cikakken, hoto mai bayani a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Bugawa yana buƙatar tsarin fayil wanda ke goyan bayan tsarin fayil iri ɗaya, ba tare da sake rubuta manyan hanyoyin haɗin yanar gizo ba tare da aiki tare na fayil mai dorewa. rundunan POSIX kuma suna buƙatar daidaita tsarin gudanarwa; a kan Windows, Node na iya ƙin hannun adireshi, don haka OmniRoute ya watsar da fayil ɗin da aka buga kuma yana ɗaukar daidaitawar shigarwar directory azaman mafi kyawun ƙoƙari. Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka ɗora ba zai iya ba da waɗannan garantin ba, farawa ya kasa rufe kafin amfani da ƙaura. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa ƙarar tare da waɗancan abubuwan farko; kar a yi amfani da `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don ketare amincin ƙaura.

### Al'amura

| Halin yanayi                | Kanfigareshan                                                                    |
| --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ci gaban gida**           | Bar duk abubuwan da ba a so. DB tana zaune a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.        |
| **Docker**                  | `DATA_DIR=/data` + ya hau ƙarar a `/data`.                                       |
| **Rufaffen lokacin hutawa** | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + kiyaye ajiyar maɓalli! Rasa shi = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Testing**              | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — ephemeral, babu boye-boye da ake bukata.        |

---

## 3. Network & Ports

| Mai canzawa                                 | Tsohuwar                                     | Fayil na asali                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                      | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar jiragen ruwa na duka Dashboard UI da API (yanayin tashar tashar guda ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(Ba komai tushen = )_                       | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | URL subpath for serving OmniRoute behind a reverse proxy (sets Next.js `basePath`; auth redirects are basePath-aware). E.g. `/omniroute`. In Docker the value is baked during `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); pre-built root images can apply a different runtime value once at container start before Next.js boots. Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` to the public origin including the same subpath.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(Ba komai tushen = )_                       | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Madubin mai lilo na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`, wanda aka lika a lokacin ginawa don haka nunin ƙarshen dashboard yana nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Faɗuwa zuwa `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` lokacin da ba a saita ba. Sake ginawa bayan canzawa ( `basePath` na gaba shine lokacin ginawa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(Ba za'a iya saita = )_                     | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Ci gaba da shigar iframe na shafukan HTML. Ba a saita ba, kowace hanya tana jigilar `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita zuwa `vscode` don hidimar shafukan (dashboard, login, docs, landing) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma babu `X-Frame-Options`, don haka lambar VS Mai Sauƙaƙan Browser na iya sanya su (yanayin OmniCopilot's `dashboardOpen: "editor"` ). Fuskar API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, laƙabin matakin tushen) yana kiyaye tsattsauran rubutun ko wace hanya. `vscode` kawai aka gane — `1`/`true` ba sa kunna shi. Gina-lokaci: sake ginawa bayan canzawa (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don hotuna; saita shi akan shigarwa da aka riga aka gina ba shi da wani tasiri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Lokacin da aka saita, yana hidimar `/v1/*` proxy API (da Gemini-native `/v1beta/*`) akan wannan keɓantaccen tashar jiragen ruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Daure adireshin tashar tashar API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Lokacin da aka saita, yana hidimar Dashboard UI akan wannan keɓantaccen tashar jiragen ruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(an kashe)_                             | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Haɗin tattaunawa a cikin jirgin sama lokaci guda; buƙatun sama da hula samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Integer mai kyau yana ba da damar gadi; unset/`0` yana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Tsayayyen ID na musamman don wannan ƙofa lokacin ɗaure misalin OmniRoute. Yana ba da damar dubawar madauki mai shigowa. Haruffa masu izini: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; matsakaicin haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | OmniRoute tushe mai URLs wanda zai iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. | `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                          | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Matsakaicin adadin OmniRoute da aka ziyarta a baya akan buƙatar da aka ɗaure (`1`-`32`). Maimaita al'amura ko gajiyawar kasafin kuɗi HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                      | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Tashar tashar jiragen ruwa don sabar sa ido na WebSocket na ainihi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                  | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Daure adireshin don uwar garken WebSocket kai tsaye.
