| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Karin asalin waƙafi da aka ba da izinin buɗe WebSocket mai rai. An riga an ba da izinin asalin dashboard ɗin ta tsohuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Karin sunayen waƙafi da aka ware don asalin WebSocket. Ba kamar `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (cikakken asalin URLs), yayi daidai da ɓangaren mai masaukin baki — mai amfani ga saitin LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL na jama'a don dashboard ɗin kai tsaye WebSocket (gefen mai lilo). Saita lokacin gaba da uwar garken WS tare da wakili na baya ko Cloudflare Tunnel (misali `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); burauzar tana haɗa wurin maimakon `ws://hostname:20132`. Hakanan ana amfani da ɓangaren hanyar suna azaman hanyar haɓaka WebSocket (tsoho: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                       | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` a `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`            | Saita zuwa `0` ko `false` don musaki sabar WebSocket ainihi (wanda aka kunna ta tsohuwa, mai ɗaure madauki). jujjuya CI/harness wanda ke hana rubutun mataimaki WebSocket kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                         | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Per- (alama, IP) iyakar saurin gudu, requests/minute. A cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya, kowane misali. `0` ko korau yana kashe ƙofar girman IP (kowane-alamar DB iyaka har yanzu tana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                 | Next.js core                                                             | Yana sarrafa maganganun shiga, caching, fallasa dalla-dalla kuskure, da inganta Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | _(Ba a saita ba)_ (zaɓin ginawa ta atomatik) | `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs`                                  | Don `npm run build`, unset ko komai yana zaɓar fakitin gidan yanar gizo lokacin da ingantaccen iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya ( RAM na zahiri ko ƙaramin cgroup/process keɓaɓɓu) ya kasance aƙalla.** 16 GiB** ko ba za a iya tabbatar da aminci ba; sama da 16 GiB, an zaɓi Turbopack. Fakitin yanar gizo na dakarun `0` ; bayyane `1` da sauran marasa sifili maras amfani suna tilasta Turbopack. Ƙimar mahalli mara komai wanda Rubuce ke gabatarwa yayi** ba** tilasta Turbopack. Wannan madaidaicin ya fito ne daga 15.6 GiB ginawa-memories regression (#18184); ginin rundunar da 24/32 GiB ba a yi masa alama ba. Samar da fakitin gidan yanar gizon yana tsallake ma'ajin sa ta atomatik ta tsohuwa (duba `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE` da ke ƙasa; #18141) kuma yana ginawa da ƙarancin kololuwar RSS fiye da hanyar Turbopack na yanzu. Ci gaba yana riƙe da halin ɗabi'a na yanzu.**Docker ya riga ya gina tsoho zuwa `0` (pack web)** ; yi amfani da `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` don shiga. Duba `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `next.config.mjs`                                                        | Samar da fakitin gidan yanar gizo yana ginawa (`OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0`) tsallake fakitin gidan yanar gizon ma'ajin tsarin fayil na dindindin sai dai idan wannan shine `1`. Rubuta wannan cache yana ƙara kusan 2.5 GB na ƙwaƙwalwar ƙira mafi girma a 6144 MB heap cap, kuma tsaftataccen gini baya karantawa. Saita zuwa `1` don kiyaye `.build/next/cache/webpack` don saurin maimaita ginawa akan injin tare da RAM don adanawa; Docker yana ginawa ɗaukar shi azaman `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE=1` (matakan maginin `Dockerfile` da `Dockerfile.bun` ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake gwajin amincin SQLite akan farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin boot akan manyan bayanan bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | systemd (sd_notify yarjejeniya)                                          | Saita ta systemd lokacin da tsari ke gudana ƙarƙashin sashin sabis tare da haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Mai amfani bai taɓa saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Saita zuwa `1` don kashe systemd sd_notify (Nau'in = notify / WatchdogSec=) koda lokacin yana gudana ƙarƙashin naúrar tsarin. Mai sanar da shi babu-op a waje tsarin ko da kuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                                    | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Tazarar (ms) don mai tsara tsarin tantance lafiyar bayanan bayanan. Mafi qarancin: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                     | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) don yanayin kiwon lafiya da aka adana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                      | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin baya-bayan nan na haɗin yanar gizo. Masu samar da bincike (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) koyaushe ana cire su daga share — "tabbatar" su "tabbatarsu" ita ce ainihin tambayar da aka biya ta sama, don haka ba a taɓa bincikar lafiyar su akan mai ƙidayar lokaci (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                          | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Saita zuwa `1` don ba da damar ingantaccen mai kira ya nemi `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, wanda ke kwatanta saman da aka kammala sau ɗaya a kowane TTL. Masu kiran da ba a san sunansu ba ba su taɓa kunna binciken ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                                       | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Matsakaicin tsayawa (ms) don rikodin madauki na taron-madauki: daskarewa fiye da wannan rajistan ayyukan layin `event loop stalled` guda ɗaya a ci gaba tare da ƙididdige ƙididdigewa a cikin jirgin, kuma yana cin karo da `eventLoopStall` counter/maximum akan `/api/monitoring/health`. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa; ƙimar da ba za a iya karantawa ba ko mara kyau tana komawa zuwa `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                    | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Daure adireshin uwar garken Next.js dev/start. Yana soke tsohuwar `0.0.0.0` lokacin da aka saita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                  | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adireshin ɗaure da mai gudu Playwright ke amfani dashi lokacin ƙaddamar da Next.js. Matsalolin zuwa `127.0.0.1` don gwaje-gwajen hermetic.** Kada ku yi amfani da `omniroute serve`** — yi amfani da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` maimakon (POSIX harsashi auto saita `HOSTNAME` zuwa sunan injin; `.env` ba zai iya soke shi ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                    | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Daure adireshin `omniroute serve`. Yana guje wa karo tare da madaidaicin POSIX harsashi `HOSTNAME` (ko da yaushe ana saita zuwa sunan injin ta bash/zsh). Faɗuwa zuwa `0.0.0.0` lokacin da ba a saita ba. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Hanyoyin Port

```
┌─────────────────────────── Single Port (default) ──────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Split Ports ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Use case: Expose API to LAN while restricting Dashboard to localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker Production ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Maps container ports to host ports in docker-compose.prod.yml.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Tsaro & Tabbatarwa

| Mai canzawa                                 | Tsoho                                                                                                 | Fayil ɗin Asali                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                 | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Gishirin da ake haɗawa da alamomin kayan aiki don ƙirƙirar sawun na’ura. A canza shi ga kowane turawar tsarin domin ware su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(ba a saita ba = gishiri na bazuwar kowane girki da ake adanawa a `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Gishirin HMAC don samar da alamar tantancewar CLI ta gida. Saita wannan ƙima yana sabunta dukkan alamomin CLI da ke kan na’urar, kuma koyaushe yana da fifiko a kan gishirin da aka adana. Duba `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                               | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Yana saita alamar `Secure` a kan kukis na zaman zama. **Dole ne ya zama `true`** idan ana aiki a bayan HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                               | API middleware                                           | Lokacin da aka kunna, abokin ciniki API proxy yana buƙatar takaddun shaida don `/api/v1/*` da `/v1/*`, gami da `GET /v1/models` a gaban mai sarrafa sa. Takaddun shaida suna bin manufofin API na abokin ciniki (maɓallan API ko ingantaccen zama). Katalogin kuma yana da rajistan mai sarrafa `requireAuthForModels`, wanda ba zai iya ƙetare wakili ba. Tare da kashe tutar duniya, kundin yana iya komawa `401`; cewa mayar da martani kadai ba ya tabbatar da inference an kare. Duba `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695). Ya yarda da `true`, `1` ko `yes`, yin watsi da harka da wuraren da ke kewaye; duk wata kima (ciki har da `on`) a kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                               | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Yana ba da damar nuna cikakkun ƙimomin maɓallan API a cikin Dashboard UI. Ana iya daidaita shi daga Feature Flags na Dashboard; haɗarin tsaro ne a kan misalan da ake rabawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(empty)_                                                                                             | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID ɗin maɓallan API da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ke tsallake rajistar buƙatu (don bin ƙa’idodin GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(unset = unlimited)_                                                                                 | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Kasafin buƙatun kowace rana na madadin da ake amfani da shi ga maɓallan API waɗanda ginshiƙin `rate_limits` ɗinsu yake null. Idan ba a saita shi ba ko kuma babu komai: babu iyaka ta atomatik (#2289, #11017). `0` ma yana nufin babu iyaka. Ingantaccen cikakken lamba N yana kunna N/rana, 5N/mako, 20N/wata. Ƙimomin da ba su da inganci kuma ba komai ba ne suna komawa ga tsoffin tagogin 1000/rana, 5000/mako, 20000/wata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Tsohuwar ƙima don saitin `maxBodySizeMb` da ake iya gani; saitin da aka adana yana da fifiko. `/api/v1/chat/completions` da `/api/v1/responses` suna riƙe tsohuwar ƙimar 50 MB har sai an adana iyaka; bayan an adana iyaka, tana maye gurbin tsohuwar ƙimar, ko sama ko ƙasa, ga mai tsaron taken da kuma tantance shigar da chat bisa ainihin adadin bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Jikunan buƙatu na ainihi da suka kai ko suka wuce wannan ƙofa suna ɗaukar hayar karɓar manyan buƙatu ta atomatik, wadda ta keɓanta da tsarin aiki na cikin gida, kafin a tantance JSON (hanyar BYTE, har da `POST /v1/responses`). Haka kuma ana amfani da hanyar tserewa ta [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) don samun isasshen sarari, kamar yadda yake ga abubuwan da ke da nauyi ta fuskar tsari; amma har yanzu `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ne ke iyakance shi, don kada [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ya sake dawowa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙaƙƙarfan iyakar hanyar chat da ake tilastawa bisa bytes ɗin da aka karanta yayin karɓa mai iyaka, har da buƙatun da `Content-Length` ya ɓace, bai inganta ba, ko kuma ba ya faɗin gaskiya; abin da ya wuce iyakar yana karɓar `413`. Saitin `maxBodySizeMb` da aka adana yana maye gurbin tsohuwar ƙimar 50 MB; idan an saita wannan canjin, shi ma yana iyakance ƙimar da aka adana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(ba a saita ba — babu iyakar adadin buƙatu)_                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** wannan tsohuwar iyakar COUNT na buƙatu yanzu tana aiki ne kawai idan an saita ta a sarari. Idan ba a saita ta ba (tsoho), ana sarrafa shigar da tattaunawa mai nauyi ta hanyar `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — kasafin BYTE da ake ƙayyade ta atomatik, wanda aka tsara daga ainihin iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiyar tsarin a cikin **tsari guda** (tari ɗaya na V8). Buƙatun `/v1/responses` guda biyu masu kusan alamomin 750k da suka zo a lokaci guda suna iya katse tarin Gi ~12 (#7849) — wannan gargaɗi ne na **kasafin ƙwaƙwalwa**, ba iyakar samfur mai tsauri ta 2 ba. Tsari mai koshin lafiya (tarin heap ɗinsa yana ƙasa da rabon zubar da kaya) IYA shigar da ƙarin buƙatun `/v1/responses` masu tsawo a lokaci guda ta hanyar `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Abokan ciniki masu tsawon SSE da yawa (40–50) suna da alaƙa da heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ba “max 2” ba. Ƙara wannan a makance zuwa “amfani da host” yana sake haifar da #7849. A ninka **heaps** da **N `DATA_DIR`s masu zaman kansu** (#11024); kada a taɓa amfani da `replicas>1` a kan fayil ɗin SQLite guda.                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(ana ƙayyade ta atomatik)_                                                                           | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** madadin kasafin bytes na shigarwa da ake ƙayyade ta atomatik (25% na mafi ƙanƙanta daga iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar V8/cgroup, a raba da ninkawar wucin-gadi ta 8x). Ana takaita ƙimomin da aka samo da waɗanda aka saita a sarari tsakanin 8 MiB–2 GiB. Jikin da ya fi kasafin da ake aiki da shi girma yana gaza nan take da `413 body_exceeds_budget`; fafatawa tsakanin jikkuna waɗanda kowannensu zai iya aiki tana ci gaba da zama `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa. Abokan ciniki 40–50 masu tsawon SSE a lokaci guda suna da alaƙa da wannan kasafi + heap, ba “max 2” mai tsauri ba. Karanta `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` a `/api/monitoring/health` kafin daidaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Rabon zubar da kaya saboda matsin heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) don shigar da buƙatu masu nauyi na BYTE da STRUCTURE (#10183, #10268, #10437). Buƙatar mai nauyi da ke gudana a lokaci guda bayan `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` za a zubar da ita ne kawai da `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa idan heap ɗin ma yana a wannan rabo ko sama da shi; a kan heap mai koshin lafiya, ana shigar da ita ta hanyar healthy-headroom maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (tsoho `1`)                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙarin damar da aka iyakance don hanyar sauri ta heap mai koshin lafiya (#10437) a kan duka STRUCTURE da BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, har da jikkuna ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Ba tare da wannan iyakar ba, kowace buƙata mai aiki amma heap ɗinta na koshin lafiya za ta ketare tsarin shigarwa ba tare da wani iyaka ba. Da zarar wannan adadin lease masu aiki a lokaci guda ya cika ta hanyar hanyar heap mai koshin lafiya, ƙarin buƙatun aiki za su koma hanyar jira/zubar da kaya mai iyaka iri ɗaya da ake amfani da ita ƙarƙashin matsin heap na gaske. `0` yana kashe ketarewar gaba ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin saƙonni da ke sanya buƙatar hira cikin nau'in mai nauyi ko da jikinta bai kai iyakar byte ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin kayan aiki da ke sanya buƙatar hira cikin nau'in mai nauyi ko da jikinta bai kai iyakar byte ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                               | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙiyasin token na girman kirtani na taka-tsantsan da ke sanya buƙata cikin nau'in mai nauyi; wannan wakili ne na kuɗin karɓar buƙata, ba tokenization ɗin lissafin kuɗin mai samarwa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (disabled)                                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iyakar tarihin hira ta zaɓi wadda ake kunna da gangan. A kashe take ta tsohuwa: adadin saƙonni wata manufa ce ta tsarin turawa, ba wata sifa ta gama-gari ta buƙata ba, kuma takaita shi a nan yana ƙin tattaunawar da kuskuren ƙarshe `413` kafin bututun matsawa ya iya sanya su masu iya aiki. `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` da tsarin rage matsin heap suna iyakance haɓakar heap. Saita ƙima mai kyau a tsarin turawa mai ƙarancin ƙwaƙwalwa wanda ke buƙatar tsayayyen iyaka; abin da ya wuce iyakar zai karɓi `413` mai tsari na compact-required.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                    | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Iyakar girma ta ƙarshe ga amsar upstream mara streaming da ake adanawa gaba ɗaya a ƙwaƙwalwa. Idan ta wuce wannan, za a soke mai karanta upstream kuma buƙatar ta gaza nan take maimakon ci gaba da faɗaɗa string mara iyaka har sai heap ya ƙare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                                 | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Matsakaicin adadin bytes na wire da ake turawa daga headers na amsar upstream. Idan an wuce kasafin, ana cire headers masu ƙarancin fifiko (misali, na musamman `x-codex-*`, `x-oai-request-id`) don kasancewa cikin iyakokin headers da reverse-proxy na gama-gari ke da su. Saita shi sama don tura ƙarin metadata na upstream, amma hakan zai ƙara girman response headers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_RECOVERY_MAX_PER_KEY`      | `64`                                                                                                  | `src/lib/quota/quotaSessionRecovery.ts`                  | Yawancin tikitin dawo da ƙima (a jiran, gudana ko jiran tabbaci) maɓallin API ɗaya na iya riƙe. Da zarar maɓalli ya kasance a iyaka, sabon juyawa yana samun kuskuren ƙididdiga na asali maimakon tikitin `202` ; sake gwada juyi da aka rigaya yayi parking ya dawo tikitin sa. Ƙimar mara inganci ko mara kyau tana amfani da tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Don sabbin deployments, an fi son `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`. CORS na browser API clients masu buƙatar sadarwa daga origin daban kawai ne; rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tantance suna amfani da buƙatun same-origin tare da kariyar CSRF da aka ɗaure da session a maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(unset)_                                                                                             | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin izinin CORS da aka raba da waƙafi. | `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                               | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Hanyar musamman ta ci gaba kawai don mayar da kowane `Origin` na burauza. Kada a kunna ta a abubuwan tura aiki na haɗin gwiwa ko na samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                                | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Sunan tsohon daidaitawa. Ana karanta canjin dashboard ɗinsa (sake rubutawa daga DB) kafin wannan ƙimar muhalli; `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` a cikin ɗaya daga cikinsu yana kashe binciken mai masaukin kamar yadda `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` ke yi, ko da an adana canjin dashboard na wannan tutar a kashe; sauran ƙimomi suna barin tsaron ya dogara ga tutoci biyun da ke ƙasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                               | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Yana kashe binciken mai masaukin na tsaron URL na fita, har da toshe bayanan gajimare (dukkan 169.254.0.0/16, metadata.google.internal), a hanyoyin mai samarwa da ke bin yanayin tsaron: tabbatar da URL, gano samfurin, URL- ɗin tushe na nodes na mai samarwa (har da nodes na sake jera abubuwa na nesa), da gwajin madadin wakili, tare da sauran hanyoyin fita masu alaƙa da mai samarwa kamar debo hoto/kafofin watsa labarai daga nesa. Haka kuma yana ba da damar wuraren da webhook na sirri ke nufi, sai dai wuraren bayanan gajimare/link-local, waɗanda suke ci gaba da toshewa ko da an kunna wannan tutar. Buƙatun tattaunawa da aka aika ta `BaseExecutor.execute()` suna ci gaba da yin binciken bayanan gajimare. Masu samarwa na gida suna aiki da tsoffin ƙimomi: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (tsoho `true`) ya riga ya ba da damar URL-ɗin gida da LAN a hanyoyin tabbatarwa, ganowa, da nodes na mai samarwa; gwajin madadin wakili da wuraren webhook na sirri suna duba wannan tuta kaɗai kuma suna toshe rundunonin gida/LAN yayin da take a kashe. Yana rinjayar `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Ƙimar da aka adana daga canjin dashboard tana da fifiko kan wannan canjin. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                                | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Ba da damar URL-ɗin mai samarwa a adireshin gida/na sirri (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, kewayon sirri) don tabbatar da mai samarwa, gano samfurin, tattaunawa, da sauran hanyoyin fita masu alaƙa da mai samarwa da yanayin tsaron ke sarrafawa. **Tsoho `true`** (na fifita na gida): tsaron sai ya toshe wuraren bayanan gajimare — dukkan 169.254.0.0/16 tare da sanannun sunayen rundunar bayanai (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Saita `false` don yanayin na jama'a kawai, wanda ke toshe rundunonin sirri da na loopback ma. Tsaron yana bincika sunan runduna ko adreshin IP kamar yadda aka rubuta, kafin kowane binciken DNS; a yanayin na jama'a kawai, abin rufe amintaccen debo yana kuma warware sunan rundunar sannan ya ƙi duk wata amsa ta sirri. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yana kashe waɗannan bincike a hanyoyin da layinsa ya jera. Masu samarwa na gida da aka gina a ciki, kamar LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, da Triton, suna tsallake duk binciken runduna don tabbatar da maɓalli da tattaunawa. Ƙimar da aka adana daga canjin dashboard tana da fifiko kan wannan canjin. (#5066)                                     |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Ba wa hanyoyin `/v1/audio/*` (rubuce-rubuce daga sauti, magana, fassarori) damar amfani da kumburin mai bayarwa mai dacewa da OpenAI da aka shirya a wajen localhost. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura sauti zuwa wani masauki na nesa yana canza asalin fitowar zirga-zirga, don haka dole ne mai gudanarwa ya yanke wannan shawarar a sarari. Ana ba da izinin kumbunan loopback/masu zaman kansu (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) koyaushe kuma wannan bai shafe su ba. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Ba wa `POST /v1/rerank` (da matakin rerank na loopback na injin ƙwaƙwalwa) damar amfani da kumburin mai bayarwa mai dacewa da OpenAI da aka shirya a wajen localhost — kwamfuta ta LAN ko abokin Tailscale da ke gudanar da TEI, Infinity, vLLM, da sauransu. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura zuwa wani masauki na nesa yana canza asalin fitowar zirga-zirga, don haka dole ne mai gudanarwa ya yanke wannan shawarar a sarari. Ana ba da izinin kumbunan loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) koyaushe kuma wannan bai shafe su ba. Dole ne kuma URL na tushe na kumburin nesa ya bi manufar URL ɗin fita ta mai bayarwa (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(komai)_                                                                                             | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Sunayen masauki (wanda waƙafi ko sarari ke raba) waɗanda tura kumburin mai bayarwa ke ɗauka a matsayin na gida — yawanci sunan sabis ɗin Docker/Compose na reranker, TEI ko audio sidecar a kan hanyar sadarwar Docker ta OmniRoute (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), ta yadda kumburin ba ya buƙatar adireshin kwantena da aka rubuta kai tsaye. Sunayen masaukin da aka jera sun cancanci `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` da binciken lafiyar gida ba tare da kunna `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` ga kowane kumburin nesa ba, kuma ana kiran su kai tsaye — ba ta `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ko wakilin da aka kulle na haɗi ba. Har yanzu suna aika maɓallin API na haɗin. Ana karɓar sunaye masu lakabi guda kawai da sunaye na `.internal`/`.local`; FQDN na jama'a, IP literals da sunayen metadata na girgije ana watsi da su tare da gargaɗi. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                               | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Lokacin da aka kunna OIDC, a kashe shiga ta kalmar sirri domin masu amfani su iya tantance kansu ta OIDC Single Sign-On kawai. Ana kuma karɓar laƙabin `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` mara tushe; Tutar Siffofin Dashboard mai wannan maɓalli tana da fifiko. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                               | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Sauƙaƙƙen laƙabin `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_MFA`                     | `false`                                                                                               | `src/server/auth/dashboardMfa.ts`                        | Maido da damar shiga bayan rasa na'urar don shigar dashboard mai matakan tabbatarwa biyu na TOTP da aka zaɓa. Lokacin da yake `true`, ana tsallake mataki na biyu ne kawai idan shigarwar ta fito daga host ɗin kansa (abokin loopback da `Host` na loopback); shigarwa daga nesa da `POST /api/cli/connect` daga wasu tushe har yanzu suna buƙatar lambar. Shiga a cikin gida, kashe tabbatarwa biyu a ƙarƙashin Saituna → Tsaro (ana iya barin lambar a sarari yayin da wannan yake a kunne, don a iya cire wani abu da ba ya sake tabbatarwa; har yanzu ana buƙatar kalmar sirri), sannan a cire shi. TOTP yana rufe shigarwa da kalmar sirri da `omniroute connect` kawai — shigarwar OIDC/SSO tana dogara ne da matakin tabbatarwa biyu na mai bayar da shaida da kansa. Duba [AUTHZ_GUIDE](../architecture/AUTHZ_GUIDE.md#optional-totp-two-factor-login-16619).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Jerin Binciken Ƙarfafa Tsaro

```bash
# Mafi ƙarancin tsaro don samarwa:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Yana buƙatar HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Tabbatar da duk kiran proxy
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Kada a taɓa bayyana maɓallan a UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Iyakar 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Sanitization & PII Kariya

OmniRoute tana ba da tsaro mai Layer biyu: na'urar duba allura ta gefen buƙatun da tsiri-gefen PII.

> **Iyakance ⚠️ :** Waɗannan hanyoyin kariya sune abubuwan gano _mafi kyawun ƙoƙarin heuristic_, ba cikakken tsarin wutan allura da sauri ba ko tsarin PII DLP. Za su iya samar da ingantattun abubuwan karya (mai kyau persona/RPG ya sa tuta) da kuma rashin kuskure (leetspeak, tazara, tsarin da ba na Ingilishi ba). Ba su isa su kadai ba don biyan bukata. Kunna hanyoyin kuma gwada zirga-zirgar zirga-zirgar ku kafin dogaro da su.

### Bangaren Buƙatu: Mai gadin allura da sauri

| Mai canzawa                       | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kunna duba saƙonnin masu shigowa don tsarin alluran gaggawa.                                                                                                                                              |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Manufar allura: log ɗin `warn` = kawai, `block` = ƙin yarda da buƙata tare da 400. Legacy `redact` yayi** ba** rubutun allurar tsiri; yi amfani da `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` don buƙatun PII sake rubutawa. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Legacy alias ga `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — hali iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`            | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Mafi ƙarancin tsananin da `MODE=block` ya ƙi: `high` (tsoho), `medium`, ko `low`. Matsakaicin alamu ana lura da su-kawai sai an saukar da su.                                                             |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Legacy alias ga `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — hali iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                        |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Lokacin da `true`, sake kunna PII a cikin buƙatun masu shigowa (wanda ya bambanta da yanayin allura).                                                                                                     |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Sake sanannun maɓallan API-maɓalli / alamar-asiri daga abubuwan biya na request/response. Ficewa; madubi `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                                         |

### Response-Side: PII Sanitizer

| Mai canzawa                      | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali            | Bayani                                                              |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Bincika amsoshin LLM don leaked PII kafin komawa ga abokin ciniki.  |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = mask PII, `warn` = log kawai, `block` = sauke duka amsa. |

### VS Code Tokenized-Hanyar Sanitizer Context

| Mai canzawa                         | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`      | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Yanke mahallin madaidaicin aiki-edita (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) daga buƙatun `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` kuma yana gyara abun ciki na fayiloli masu mahimmanci a haɗe. Amintaccen-ta-tsohuwar; saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. |

### Al'amura

| Halin yanayi           | Kanfigareshan                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Yarda da kasuwanci** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (tubalan allura + request/response PII redaction; hanyoyin suna zaman kansu) |
| **Saka idanu kawai**   | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — rajistan ayyukan amma ba tare da toshewa                                                                                                    |
| **Amfani na sirri**    | Bar duk nakasassun — sifili a sama                                                                                                                                                                        |
