## 6. Ka'idojin Kayan aiki & Hanyar Hanya

| Mai canzawa                                                 | Tsoho                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                               | Yana sarrafa damar LLM na amfani da kayan aiki/kiran ayyuka. `allowlist` = kayan aikin da aka jera kawai, `denylist` = duk kayan aikin ban da waɗanda aka jera, `disabled` = babu takurawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`                   | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin JSON mai ƙa’idojin sarrafa payload (gyare-gyaren upstream na kowane samfurin/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`                   | Tazarar sake loda fayil ɗin ƙa’idojin payload (a millisecond) domin sabunta shi yayin aiki. Mafi ƙanƙanta ita ce `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                          | Zaɓi na son rai: yana tura ID ɗin samfurin `claude-*` marar prefix daga abokan cinikin Claude Code ta asusun Claude Code OAuth maimakon buƙatar prefix na mai samarwa. Bayyanannun prefix na mai samarwa har yanzu su ne ke da fifiko. Hakanan ana iya saita shi ta hanyar maballin kunnawa/kashewa a dashboard ɗin shafin mai samar da Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                       | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts`       | Saita shi zuwa `false` domin dakatar da OmniRoute daga ƙara beta na Anthropic `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` da kansa (tubalan tunani marasa sa hannu sai kawai). Buƙatun da ke da `thinking.display: "summarized"` suna tsallake shi ko da ya ya kasance; beta na redact da abokin ciniki ya yi shawararsa ko zaɓin `requestDefaults.redactThinking` mai dacewa da CC yana ci gaba da kunna shi. Buƙatun Claude OAuth suna riƙe beta ɗin (yana cikin tambarin Claude Code OAuth) sai dai idan an kunna tutar fasalin `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED`, wadda a tsoho take a kashe.                                                              |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                          | Halayyar tsohuwar `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): tana maye gurbin duk wani sunan samfurin da ba shi da prefix da `defaultModel` na haɗin da aka zaɓa. A tsoho tana a kashe; `auto` ko samfurin da babu komai ne kawai ake maye gurbinsu, kuma tana rubuta gargaɗi guda ɗaya ga kowane ma’aurata idan an kunna ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`                    | Iyakar yawan buƙatun lokaci guda ga kowane samfurin a cikin combos na round-robin (#9100). An takaita semaphore na combo na round-robin zuwa buƙatu 3 masu gudana lokaci guda ga kowane samfurin, ba tare da wata damar sauya hakan ba, wanda ke jera zirga-zirgar da ke da babban concurrency a bayan wannan iyaka. An tabbatar da ƙimarsa ta zama `>= 1`, sannan an takaita ta zuwa `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENTIC_STALL_FAILOVER`                          | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/combo/agenticStallFailover.ts`     | Zaɓi na son rai: saita zuwa `1` (ko `true`) domin idan wani memba na combo ya amsa wani juyi na sakamakon kayan aiki da `finish: stop` + bayani + sifili na kiran kayan aiki, a koma ga manufa ta gaba (dabarun madaukin fifiko, round-robin da zaman da aka kulle). Idan babu wata manufa da ta yi fiye, za a mayar da amsar da ta tsaya maimakon kuskure, kuma ba za a ɗora wa mai katsewar mai samarwa ko kulle samfurin wani caji ba. Ana riƙe juyawa masu yawo daga abokin ciniki har sai an kammala hukunci. Ba a rarraba buƙatun da ke amfani da siffar `input` ta Responses API ba. A kashe ta tsohuwa.                                             |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                       | Zaɓi mai haɗari na son rai da ke tsallake binciken OmniRoute na gida kan iyakar mahallin / iyakar alamomin shigarwa don buƙatun kai tsaye na samfurin guda. Masu samarwa na sama har yanzu suna tilasta ainihin iyakokinsu; matsa prompt da iyakar alamomin fitarwa ta samfurin da kansa suna nan aiki. Tsarin fifiko mai aiki shi ne: canjin Feature Flags DB > canjin muhalli > tsohuwa; ba a buƙatar sake farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Takardar YAML ta layi cikin `providers:` (RIC-738, D4). Idan an saita ta (tare da ko ba tare da tubalin `strategy:` ba), `/v1/chat/completions` yana karkata zuwa shigarwar OpenAI mai jituwa ta haɗin kai da ake karɓar baƙuncinta da kansa, maimakon tsarin cloud. Idan ba a saita ta ba (tsohuwa): hanyar tana wucewa kai tsaye zuwa tsarin cloud ɗin da yake akwai. Duba `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Hanyar fayil ɗin YAML da ke ɗauke da takardar `providers:` iri ɗaya da ta `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, don tura tsarin da suka fi son fayil maimakon canjin muhalli na layi. Kowannensu yana kunna shigarwar da ake karɓar baƙuncinta da kanta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(unset — open route)_       | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`                | Maɓallin API na zaɓi da aka raba don shigarwar da ake karɓar baƙuncinta da kanta (tsarin D5, an tanada shi don tsarin ƙayyade adadin kowane maɓalli). Idan an saita shi, buƙatu dole su haɗa da `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Idan ba a saita shi ba: hanyar a buɗe take, daidai da tsarin mai samarwa na gida da ake karɓar baƙuncinsa da kansa da ke akwai (tura shi a loopback/cibiyar sadarwa amintacciya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`              | Takardar YAML ta layi cikin `strategy:` don injin karkatarwa mai ƙayyadaddun sakamako (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, mai katsewa mai lokacin sanyi, fifikon kuɗi, mai lura da jinkiri, sarkar madadin. Yana rinjayar tubalin `strategy:` na layi da aka saka cikin `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, bisa kowane maɓalli. Duba `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`              | Hanyar fayil ɗin YAML da ke ɗauke da takardar `strategy:` iri ɗaya da ta `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, don tura tsarin da suka fi son fayil maimakon canjin muhalli na layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`            | Saita `1` domin dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana mayar da sakamakon da ba a bi diddigi ba kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya fassara tarihin saƙonni, kuma hakan ya shafi ID na zaman da abokin ciniki ya bayar ma. Gudanar da zaman karkatarwa bai canza ba, kuma ba a goge bayanan da suke akwai ba. Don tsarin da ba ya amfani da kallon tattaunawa na dashboard kuma yake son a dakatar da ƙaruwa na teburin juyawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`        | Maɓallin API don dabarar mai ba da hanya ta auto-combo ta `nadir` wadda aka zaɓa da gangan, ana amfani da shi lokacin da `config.nadir.apiKey` na combo ɗin kansa bai saita ba. Zaɓi ne: kira ba tare da maɓalli ba yana shiga matakin Nadir na anonymous, wanda ake iyakance adadinsa bisa IP, don haka ba ya aiki a bayan ƙofa ta raba-gateway. Mai muhimmanci; an fi so a yi amfani da filin na kowane combo lokacin da combo-oli ke amfani da asusu daban-daban.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`        | Tushen adireshin asalin API ɗin yanke shawara na Nadir, ana amfani da shi lokacin da `config.nadir.baseUrl` na combo ɗin kansa bai saita ba. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don Nadir da aka shirya a kan uwar garken kanka, wanda ke ajiye farkon prompt a cikin hanyar sadarwarka. Ana jure `/v1` a ƙarshen adireshin kuma a cire shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`                  | Tagar mako-mako don iyakokin amfani na USD na kowane maɓallin API (tilasta buƙata, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: makon yana ƙarewa ne a farkon lokacin sake saiti na mako-mako daga cikin haɗin upstream da maɓallin zai iya isa gare su, ko kuma ya zama kwanaki 7 masu juyawa idan ba a san kowane lokaci ba. `calendar`: makon gida daga Litinin 00:00 zuwa Litinin ta gaba 00:00, iri ɗaya ga kowane maɓalli. Ana ba da shawarar `calendar` lokacin da rukunin asusun upstream guda ɗaya ake rabawa tsakanin maɓallan da aka ware wa kasafin kuɗi daban-daban. Ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba suna komawa zuwa `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(lokacin yankin process)_   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`                  | Yankin lokaci na IANA (misali `Asia/Shanghai`) wanda ke bayyana Litinin 00:00 don tagar mako-mako ta `calendar`. Idan ba a saita shi ba ko kuma bai inganta ba: ana amfani da yankin lokacin aikin OmniRoute (wato `TZ` na host ko container, wanda sau da yawa ba a saita shi ba, wato Coordinated Universal Time, a cikin hotunan container). Ana yin watsi da shi a yanayin `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                        | Tsohuwar iyakar adadin tools da ake bari a cikin buƙata (tsohuwar OpenAI). Lambobi masu kyau kawai; idan ba a saita ƙimar ba ko kuma ba ta inganta ba, ana riƙe `128`. Mai bayarwa mai sanannen/tsayayyen iyaka (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) har yanzu yana amfani da nasa iyakar — wannan yana matsar da rufin tsoho ne kawai. Target-oli masu tsarin Claude ba su da tsohuwar iyaka (Messages API ba shi da ita); iyakar da aka koya har yanzu tana aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_COUNT`                     | `16`                         | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts`     | Amsoshi zuwa fassarar Chat: adadin hotunan fitarwar tool (`function_call_output`, `custom_tool_call_output`, rawar `tool`) da ake sake aikawa a matsayin sassan hoton mai amfani a kowace buƙata. Ana riƙe sababbi, kuma kowane tsoho daga cikinsu ya zama madadin rubutu mai ɗauke da `tool_call_id`. Lambobi masu kyau kawai; idan ba a saita ƙimar ba ko kuma ba ta inganta ba, ana riƙe `16`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `20971520` (20 MB)           | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts`     | Jimillar girman URL-olin hotunan fitarwar tool da ake sake aikawa a kowace buƙata a cikin wannan fassarar. Ana watsar da hotunan da suka wuce kasafin kuɗi, daga mafi tsufa zuwa sabo, tare da gargaɗi guda ɗaya a kowace buƙata. Lambobi masu kyau kawai; idan ba a saita ƙimar ba ko kuma ba ta inganta ba, ana riƙe `20971520`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_RESPONSES_STRICT_TOOL_HISTORY`                   | `false`                      | `open-sse/translator/helpers/responsesToolHistory.ts` | Yadda ake sarrafa tarihin kayan aiki mara daidaituwa: fitarwa ba tare da kira ba, kira ba tare da fitarwa ba, ɓacewa, mai tsayi ko mara inganci `call_id`, ko gardamar tarihi mara kyau. A kashe, OmniRoute yana gyara tarihi kuma ya aika da shi (an watsar da abubuwan marayu, kiran da ba a amsa ba yana samun fitowar komai, ids ana ƙulla su ko manne, gardama mara kyau ta zama `{}`). Kunna, buƙatar tana samun `400` kafin aikawa; ana tura ingantaccen tarihi tare da ids ɗin kiran sa da hujjojin JSON kamar yadda aka aiko. Ya yarda da `true` ko `1`; kowace ƙima tana kiyaye tsoho.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(a kunne)_                  | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`                     | Kiran tools da aka kwaikwayi a prompt na SYNTX.ai: idan ba a saita shi ba ko kuma ya kasance `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes`, ana saka kundin tools na gida cikin prompt; kowace ƙima daban (misali `0`) tana tsallake wannan sakawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## 7. URLs & Cloud Sync

| Variable                                    | Default                                                       | Source File                                                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                      | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | URL -gefen uwar garken don ayyukan daidaitawa na ciki don kiran `/api/sync/cloud`. Rike wannan azaman loopback/container URL koda lokacin da app ɗin ke da wakilci a bainar jama'a. Ƙwarewar A2A kuma suna amfani da ita (duba `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`) kuma ta kasa aiki maimakon aika maɓallin API zuwa ga jama'a-http URL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CLOUD_URL`                                 | _(empty)_                                                     | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | URL na wurin ƙarshen relay na Cloud (fasalin biyan kuɗi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `12000`                                                       | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Lokacin ƙarewa na HTTP don buƙatun aiki tare da Cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                   | `full`                                                        | Tsarin gina Webpack                                                         | Bayanan martabar lokacin ginawa (saita zuwa `minimal` don cire modules masu gata daga bundle a zahiri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Ba saitin lokacin aiki ba ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE`    | `false`                                                       | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                                                      | Saita zuwa `true` don ƙin amsar Cloud Sync da ba ta da sa hannu idan ba a saita sirrin gida ba (#13679). Ana tabbatar da sa hannun da ke akwai koyaushe — kuma koyaushe a ƙi shi idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` ba — ba tare da la’akari da wannan tuta ba. | `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                        | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`                              | Protocol ɗin da ake amfani da shi wajen samo URL ta manifest ɗin plugin na mai samarwa daga HOST/PORT idan babu asalin buƙata. | `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                             | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`                                         | Yanayin amincewa na zaɓi don headers na asalin jama'a da aka tura. Idan ba a saita ba = kada a amince da `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` wajen yanke shawarar tsaro. `true` / `loopback` suna amincewa da host/proto da aka tura ne kawai daga proxy na loopback mai tambarin token. `private` / `lan` kuma suna amincewa da abokan proxy na LAN mai zaman kansa, suna barin gadar `API_PORT` ta riƙe `X-Forwarded-For` na irin wannan abokin, kuma suna barin hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY su karɓi abokin ciniki na cibiyar sadarwa mai zaman kansa wanda irin wannan proxy ya ruwaito (ba maɓallin Cloudflare, sarkar loopback kaɗai, ko ƙofar token na CLI ta loopback kaɗai ba). | `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                            | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`                                         | Default User-Agent da ake amfani da shi lokacin da `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yake kunne kuma ba a saita override na `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` na kowane provider ba. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai ga masu aiwatar da opencode. Idan upstream ya sanya ƙofa ga buƙatar da ba ta da key, kuma ƙimar da aka saita ba ta ƙunshi `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` ba, ana maye gurbinta da wannan default maimakon a ƙi buƙatar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (on)                                                      | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`                                         | Isar da headers na danganta manhajar client (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) zuwa upstream providers, domin buƙatar da ta fara a cikin harness da aka lissafa ta riƙe asalinta ta cikin router (ƙofofin publisher na OpenRouter suna dogara da waɗannan). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` yana riƙe da asalin registry/operator kuma yana watsar da ƙimomin da client ya samar. Custom headers da operator ya saita koyaushe suna rinjayar ƙimomin client.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                     | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Ƙimar header ɗin `x-opencode-client` da aka ƙirƙira lokacin da `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yake kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                      | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                                            | Ƙimar header ɗin `x-opencode-project` da aka ƙirƙira lokacin da `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yake kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(unset)_                                                     | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`                                     | Jerin providers na ƙarshe waɗanda ake rarraba gazawar upstream ɗinsu bisa ga egress IP (429 guda ɗaya yana dakatar da kowace haɗin sibling da ke raba wannan IP). Idan ba a saita shi ba, ana amfani da tsohon saitin free-tier `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` yana kashe cascade ɗin gaba ɗaya; jerin da aka raba da waƙafi yana maye gurbin saitin. Upstream yana rarraba paid plans bisa asusu, don haka za a iya kashe shi cikin aminci (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(unset)_                                                     | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`                                     | Iyakar juyawar egress idan an ƙi saboda ƙayyadadden quota. Idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ware memban pool na ɗan lokaci ne kawai idan an samu 429 ga iyalin da aka raba bisa IP da ke sama; `all` yana faɗaɗa wannan rubutawa zuwa kowane provider, ta yadda rate limit da aka samu ta hannun wani memba zai sa zaɓi na gaba ya yi egress a wani wuri dabam. Ana watsi da kowace ƙima ban da `all`. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (a kunne ta tsohuwa). Ana tsara store bisa adireshin egress, ba (provider, address) ba, don haka 429 da aka faɗaɗa yana ajiye wannan memba ga kowane provider har lokacin ya ƙare (minti 5, yana ƙaruwa har zuwa 15). Ana karantawa bayan kowace amsar sakamako, don haka canji yana aiki ba tare da sake farawa ba. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(unset)_                                                     | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Saita zuwa `off` don dakatar da daidaita jikin buƙatun OpenCode marasa maɓalli (tutar streaming da jerin kayan aiki). Ana ci gaba da amfani da headers. Ana karantawa a kowace buƙata, don haka canji yana aiki nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(unset)_                                                     | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Sunayen kayan aiki da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a bayyana a cikin buƙatar OpenCode marar maɓalli wadda ba ta ɗauke da komai, lokacin da har yanzu ba a lura da komai ba game da wannan samfurin. Idan babu komai, ana komawa zuwa placeholder guda ɗaya wanda aka gaya wa samfurin kada ya kira shi. Ba za su wuce entries 32 ba, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; ana watsi da entries marasa inganci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_CREDENTIAL`             | `public`                                                      | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts`                            | Shaidar da ake aikawa a layin free-tier mai takura (`https://opencode.ai/inference/v1`). Tun daga 2026-10-21, wannan layin yana amsa 403 `Model access is disabled` ga ainihin API key kuma a maimakon haka yana yi wa anonymous literal hidima. `key` yana riƙe API key na mai gudanarwa, `none`/`off` ba ya aika header na credential, kowace ƙima ta literal daban kuma ana aikawa a matsayin bearer token. Ana karantawa a kowace buƙata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_RELAY_URL`              | rashin daidaitawa                                             | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Tushen URL na gudun ba da sanda na Bun don buƙatun OpenCode masu gated kyauta. Lokacin da aka saita, waɗannan buƙatun suna ɗaukar tsarin OpenCode CLI da sauri da saitin kayan aiki kuma ana aika su ta hanyar relay (`scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`), saboda sama ta amsa 429 ga abokan ciniki waɗanda ba Bun HTTP ba. Za a fara dawo da URLs tare da uwar garken lokacin da `bun` ke kan PATH; an yi watsi da wata hanya a kan URL. Ana karɓar `http://` na fili kawai don rundunonin madauki (`localhost`, `127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`); duk wani mai masaukin baki dole ne yayi amfani da `https://`, in ba haka ba buƙatun sun kasa tare da kuskure bayyananne. Kammala Taɗi kawai. Karanta kowane buƙata.                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_BUN`                   | `bun` na PATH                                                 | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Bun binary ya yi amfani da shi don fara relay.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_SCRIPT`                | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts` karkashin tushen kunshin | `src/lib/opencodeFreeRelay.ts`                                              | Hanyar rubutun relay. Ana warware matsalar tsoho daga tushen fakitin da aka shigar, sannan littafin jagorar aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_HOST`                  | `127.0.0.1`                                                   | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Saurari mai watsa shiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_RELAY_PORT`                  | `20199`                                                       | `scripts/ops/opencode-free-relay.ts`                                        | Saurari tashar jiragen ruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

OmniRoute yana aika shi a cikin `x-opencode-relay-auth`; Relay ya amsa 401 ba tare da shi ba. A sauya shi don relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

A sauya shi don relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Bayani ne mai matuƙar sirri; a fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(unset)_                                                     | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Madadin env var na cookie `__Secure-session` na Ollama Cloud. Bayani ne mai matuƙar sirri; a fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(unset)_                                                     | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Madadin canjin yanayin cookie na `__Secure-session` na Ollama Cloud da ake amfani da shi kafin gajerun laƙabban. Mai ɗauke da bayanai masu muhimmanci; an fi so a yi amfani da filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`               | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                                                | Ƙarshen API na iyakokin amfani da CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). A canja shi don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan wakili na baya (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa ga tsayayyen URL na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) lokacin da OAuth ya sake kiran waya ko ƙirƙirar hanyoyin haɗin jama'a dole ne suyi amfani da sunan mai masaukin. Idan ba tare da wannan ba, sake kiran OAuth na iya gazawa saboda redirect_uri ba zai yi daidai ba kuma hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira na iya nunawa a asalin kwantena na ciki.
>
> Rike `BASE_URL` azaman loopback/container URL na ciki don ayyukan uwar garke-zuwa uwar garke. Kar a yi amfani da `Origin` mai bincike ko sunan mai masaukin jama'a don ɗaukar bayanan kai na ciki.
>
> Rubuce-rubucen dashboard ingantattu baya buƙatar tsayayyen tushe na jama'a URL: dashboard ɗin yana aika buƙatun marasa aminci na asali iri ɗaya tare da alamar CSRF mai ɗaure. OmniRoute har yanzu tana daidaita ingantaccen asalin jama'a don haɗin haɗin yanar gizon da ba na dashboard ba: bayyane URL env vars na jama'a an amince da su da farko; raw `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` kanun labarai ba a yi watsi da su ba sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma ana buga tambarin wakilin wakilin nan da nan kamar yadda aka amince. Kar a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara buƙatun dashboard na asali iri ɗaya; CORS don abokan ciniki na tushen-tsaye ne kawai.

---

## 8. Wakili mai fita

Mai ba da sabis LLM na sama yana kira ta hanyar wakili na HTTP ko SOCKS5 don sarrafa egress, hanyar hanya, ko masking IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` ya sabawa `true`. Saita shi zuwa `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` don tilasta HTTP/1.1 akan masu aikawa Undici a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` da `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: kai tsaye egress, HTTP(S)/SOCKS proxies, relays, da sabo-sabo-socket sake gwadawa. Wannan yana adana takaddun shaida TLS, tantancewar wakili, iyakokin haɗin kai, da saituna masu rai. Sake kunna OmniRoute bayan canza shi saboda ana adana masu aikawa kuma an ƙirƙiri wakilai na relay a farawa. Sauyawa baya sarrafa wreq TLS zanen yatsan hannu ko takamaiman jigilar mai bayarwa.

| Mai canzawa                              | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kunna wakilin SOCKS5 don kira na gaba. Fita tare da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_GZIP_REQUEST_BODIES`          | `true`            | `open-sse/executors/base/gzipRequestBody.ts`   | Gzip jikin buƙatun da aka aika zuwa `api.anthropic.com` na hukuma (haruffa 4096 +, haɗin kai kai tsaye kawai; wanda aka tsallake a bayan wakili, kwaikwayo TLS ko `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`). Anthropic yana karɓar gzip kawai. Ƙimar da aka ƙi (415, 400/403 ba na ɗan adam ba, ko asalin JSON-parse 400) ana jin daɗin ba tare da matsawa sau ɗaya ba kuma asalin yana zama ba a matsawa na sa'a ɗaya ba. Fita tare da `0`, `false`, `off` ko `no`. |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Abokin ciniki-bangaren                         | Wayar da kan abokin ciniki game da samuwar SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tuta ta tsohuwa (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override yayi nasara). Wuraren wakili da jujjuyawar kowane-asusu suna dakatar da yin hidima ga memba wanda kawai ya gaza (ƙi binciken TCP, ko 429 ta hanyarsa) na tsawon lokaci wanda ya ninka akan kowane maimaitawa, har zuwa tafiya. `false` yana dawo da zaɓe a sarari.                                                                                           |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Lissafin ba da izini don maƙasudin zaɓin sarrafawa na gida-core fiye da madauki (comma/space ware runduna ko IPs, `*` yana buɗe LAN/private amma bai taɓa metadata/link-local ba). An ba da izinin dawo da baya akan http/https ta tsohuwa. Ƙimar dashboard DB tana samun nasara akan wannan canjin lokacin da aka saita kuma ba ta da komai.                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Lokacin tushe (ms) na 429 saiti, wanda aka ninka akan kowane maimaitawa. Karanta sau ɗaya a farawa. An daure zuwa `1000..3600000`; wanda ba a iya karantawa ko kuma wanda ba a iya karantawa ya faɗi baya ga tsoho tare da faɗakarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Cap (ms) na 429 saiti. Karanta sau ɗaya a farawa. An daure zuwa `1000..3600000`; wanda ba a iya karantawa ko kuma wanda ba a iya karantawa ya faɗi baya ga tsoho tare da faɗakarwa. Lokacin da hular ke ƙasa da tushe, ya faɗi baya zuwa tsoho. Rage hula kuma yana rage yawan mantuwa: shigarwar tana share `2 x max` bayan ƙarewa.                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasalin ficewa (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override wins). Ga masu samar da adadin kuɗin da adireshin egress ya cika, sanya matsayi na memba wanda ke raba adireshin ficewar memba da aka ƙi kwanan nan kusa da mambobi masu lafiya. Oda kawai, ba a cire ba. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, wanda ke haifar da siginar ƙi da yake karantawa.                                                   |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Maimaita tagar (ms) tana maimaita faɗakarwar `proxy.set_aside` webhook kowane memba na tafkin don haka fashewar ƙi ya aika taron guda ɗaya. Karanta akan kowane fitarwa. An daure zuwa `1000..3600000`; wanda ba a iya karantawa ba ko kuma ba a iya karantawa ya faɗi baya ga tsoho a shiru.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | HTTP proxy don kira na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | HTTPS proxy don kira na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | Wakilin duniya (yana goyan bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Sanya makasudin echo-IP wanda masu binciken egress ke amfani da shi zuwa URL guda ɗaya. Ba a saita ba, binciken yana gwada `api64.ipify.org` sannan `api4.ipify.org` don haka ba a ba da rahoton mutuwar IPv4-only tunnels ba (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Node.js standard                               | Waƙar hostnames/IPs don ketare wakili.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Matsakaicin soket na lokaci ɗaya a kowane cache HTTP/SOCKS wakili mai aikawa. Rafukan SSE masu tsayi kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar haɗi fiye da ɗaya lokacin da buƙatun da yawa ke raba wakili na matakin asusu ɗaya. Ƙimar da ke sama da `256` an lissafta su.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | SOCKS5 musafaha (haɗa) lokacin ƙarewa a cikin ms. Tada shi lokacin da babban gidan ƙofa guda ɗaya ya sami babban haɗin gwiwa (misali buƙatun lokaci guda 100) — ainihin musafaha na iya wuce 10s a ƙarƙashin cikakken tafkin duk da cewa ana iya isa ga wakili, wanda in ba haka ba yana sama kamar `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` na ƙarya. An buga a `120000`.                                                                           |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | When `false` (default), a request whose assigned proxy fails to resolve is **refused (fail-closed)** rather than falling back to a direct connection — prevents real-IP leaks. Set `true` to restore the legacy DIRECT fallback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Spoof TLS yatsa ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Matsalolin JA3/JA4 suna tarewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Jerin ba da izini na mai ba da waƙafi don sabon proxied TLS routing (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS yana kiyaye halayen gado lokacin da ba a saita shi ba; kawai waɗannan masu samar da hanya suna hanya ta gadar hoton yatsa Chrome-124. Groq (`api.groq.com`) ba a cire shi ko da lokacin da lissafin izinin ba a saita ba, saboda Chrome JA3 yana haifar da Cloudflare 1010 yayin da undici na asali ya kai API.                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Ba da izinin mahallin mai lilo na Claude Turnstile Playwright don yin watsi da kurakuran takardar shaidar HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Al'amura

| Halin yanayi                                      | Kanfigareshan                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 ta ramin SSH**                           | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                                 |
| **Wakilin HTTP na kamfani**                       | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`       |
| **Anti sawun yatsa**                              | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa)                                                                |
| **Sarrafa Egress / babu damar kai tsaye**         | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (default). Buƙatun sun gaza da ƙarfi lokacin da babu wakili maimakon yawo ta kai tsaye.             |
| **Legacy / dev — ba da izinin faɗuwar kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana dawo da dabi'ar taurin kai: haɗin kai tsaye da ake amfani dashi lokacin da ƙudurin wakili ya gaza. |

> **Note (NVIDIA ingantacciyar hanyar wucewa — #3226):** NVIDIA's API-key ingantacciyar madaidaicin wurin tsayawa lokacin da aka ci gaba da kawowa ta cikin proxy/TLS-patched na duniya (undic dispatcher → 504). `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` da gangan ya ketare facin wakili na waccan ingantaccen kiran ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`. Wannan a rubuce, keɓantacce keɓantacce — yana aikatawa** ba** shafi chat/usage egress. Wurin wucewa yana da iyaka ta hanyar `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗin Kayan Aikin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke ganowa da ƙaddamar da motocin gefe CLI (Lambar Claude, Codex, da sauransu).

| Mai canzawa                       | Tsohuwar                                                | Fayil na asali                                        | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

Dole ne ya zama cikakke kuma a cikin tsarin gida —** ko** , a cikin akwati, hanyar da aka ɗaure (haka `/host-home` ke aiki). Saita wannan zuwa 512000 don ƙunsar buƙatun — shirin ya ƙidaya ƙasa da shi. Ana watsi da kowace ƙima, don haka buga rubutu ba zai iya neman taga da samfurin ba ya da shi. |

Tsohuwar ita ce tagar mahallin ƙofar (1000000); wannan darajar na iya rage wannan rufin, kada ta ɗaga shi sama da taga.                                                                                                                                                  |

| `MINIMAX_CODE_PROFILE_DIR`        | `~/.minimax`                                            | `open-sse/executors/minimaxCodeGateway.ts`            | Jagorar bayanin martabar lambar MiniMax wanda ke riƙe da bayanin martaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MINIMAX_CODE_AUTH_RECORD`        | _(sabon tsara)_                                         | `open-sse/executors/minimaxCodeGateway.ts`            | Yi rikodin bayanin martaba don amfani lokacin da yake riƙe da asusu da yawa.
