Yayi daidai da env var mai sakawa Hamisu PowerShell akan Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `WHYCODES_HOME`                   | `~/.whycodes`                                           | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/whycodesHome.ts` | Yana maye gurbin tushen WhyCodes da ke ɗauke da `config.toml`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, yana nemo `.whycodes` ƙarƙashin `HOME`, sannan `USERPROFILE`, sai kuma `os.homedir()`. Ana amfani da ƙimar maye gurbin yadda take kai tsaye; ba a faɗaɗa `~` da ke farkonta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### CLI Profile Auto-Sync

Waɗannan tutocin fasalin sun fice kuma an kashe su. Hakanan ana iya kunna su daga dashboard na Code CLI.

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali                                   | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan aiki tare da samfurin mai bayarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanin `~/.codex/*.config.toml` ta atomatik daga kundin adireshi. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; baya taɓa canza saitin active/default Codex, auth, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai bayarwa.                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaitawar ƙirar mai ba da sabis, ta atomatik ta sake rubuta fayilolin bayanin martaba na lambar `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` Claude daga kundin adireshi. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; baya taɓa canza saitin active/default Claude, auth, ko zaɓin mai bayarwa. |

### Docker

```bash
# Mount host binaries into the container and tell OmniRoute where they are:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana yin tasiri ne kawai lokacin da aka ɗaure hanyar daga mai masaukin — tare da hawa kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba bayanin martabar `host` a `docker-compose.yml`). Hanyar da ba ta cikin gidan mai amfani da kwantena ba kuma ba ta da ɗaure ba, saboda za a yi watsi da rubutu a wurin lokacin da aka sake ƙirƙirar akwati.

Hoton yana gudana azaman `USER node`, don haka `/root` wanda ba a saka shi ba shine** ba** mai inganci.

| Mai canzawa                              | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(moto)_          | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Tilasta gano akwati akan (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙata kawai akan lokutan aiki kawai ganowar atomatik ya ɓace.                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Bada damar saitin kayan aiki na CLI ya rubuta cikin hanyar kwantena da ba a hawa ba ta wata hanya. Madaidaicin CLI shine `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                              |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | rashin daidaitawa | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | hoton Docker kawai: dashboard** Sabunta Yanzu** ya rubuta buƙatun JSON zuwa wannan fayil (dole ne ya kasance a cikin `DATA_DIR`) don mai kula da mai masaukin baki don ja da sake ƙirƙirar akwati. Duba [Jagorar Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### CLI Binary (`omniroute`) mataimaka

Waɗannan masu canji suna daidaita halayen binary na `omniroute` CLI (ba gano motar gefe a sama ba).

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar                          | Fayil na asali                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(tsarin)_                        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Tilasta harshen fitarwa CLI. yankin BCP-47 (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana soke tsarin gida env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don tura stdout/stderr uwar garken zuwa tasha a yanayin da ake kulawa. Daidai da tutar `--log` akan `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Alamar tabbatar da na'ura ta allurar a matsayin kai `x-omniroute-cli-token`. An ƙirƙira ta atomatik a cikin ɗawainiya 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Dagewar Gemini tushen gida; Gudun harbi ɗaya yana amfani da keɓantaccen gida na ɗan lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Lokacin alheri a cikin milli seconds kafin tilastawa-kashe yaro mai alamar CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa 1 tare da bayyananniyar DATA_DIR don tsallake tsohuwar fayil ɗin mahalli na bayanan bayanan mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Alamar daidaitawa don `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` yana karɓar alamar daidaitawa kawai, ba maɓallin API ba). Daidai da `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Ƙoƙarin ƙarewar HTTP (ms) don buƙatun sabar CLI →.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Matsakaicin lokaci (ms) CLI yana jiran ƙarshen lafiyar uwar garken kafin buga gargaɗin ƙarewar lokaci. Yana da amfani ga jinkirin farawa sanyi (misali Windows). Hakanan ana iya saitawa ta hanyar `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don buga binciken retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Littafin al'ada don gano kayan aikin CLI ( fakitin `omniroute-cmd-*` ). Tsoffin zuwa `~/.omniroute/plugins/` lokacin cirewa. CLI-kawai — bai taɓa isa zuwa na'urar daukar hotan takardu ta gefen uwar garken ba, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` ke nunawa (sashe na 2).                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(Ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Danne abubuwan dubawa lokacin da aka saita zuwa kowace ƙima mara fa'ida: sanarwar CLI a wurin fitowar tsari da banner "sabuntawa akwai" dashboard (`GET /api/system/version` sannan ya tsallake npm, GitHub da duban labarai). Hakanan sanarwar CLI tana tsayawa a cikin CI, yanayin shiru, da fitowar mara rubutu.                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(an samu)_                       | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Rayuwa URL bincike ta `omniroute doctor`. Zaɓin `livenessUrl` na shirye-shirye yana ɗaukar fifiko; in ba haka ba ana amfani da wannan darajar da baki. Ba a saita ba, CLI yana samun ƙarshen `/api/health/degradation` ɗin dashboard na gida.                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Hanyar kasidar mai bayarwa da umarnin mai bada CLI ke amfani dashi. Zaɓin `catalogPath` na shirye-shirye yana ɗaukar fifiko. Ana amfani da cikakkun hanyoyi kamar yadda yake; hanyoyin zumunta sun warware daga tushen aikin. Ba a saita ba, CLI yana amfani da kundin adireshi na masu badawa, tare da gadon fayil `providers.ts` azaman koma baya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(Tsohon mai bayarwa)_            | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Samfurin faɗuwar duniya don gwajin haɗin mai bada CLI. An saita fifikon ƙirar haɗin kai, ƙayyadaddun ƙirar mai badawa ya soke, wannan ƙimar, sannan ƙirar gwajin da aka gina a cikin mai bayarwa.                                                                                                                                                    |

---

## 10. Wakilin Cikin Gida & Haɗin kai MCP

| Variable                                        | Default                                             | Source File                                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | auto-ganewa                                         | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Bayyanar URL don kayan aikin MCP/A2A don isa OmniRoute. Yana soke ganowa ta atomatik localhost. Ƙwarewar A2A ta gaza maimakon aika maɓalli API zuwa wannan URL (ko `BASE_URL` ya koma baya) lokacin da yake bayyana a fili http zuwa adireshin jama'a, sunan mai masaukin jama'a mai dige-dige ko ma'aikacin girgije-metadata, ko yana riƙe da takaddun shaida: yi amfani da https, ko http zuwa madauki, akwati ko sunan sabis, ko adireshin sirri.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                   | Farashin MCP/A2A                                            | Maɓallin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki. Ƙwarewar A2A tana aika shi kawai don mai kira ba tare da maɓallin API ba (zaman dashboard); mai kira mai maɓalli ya yi da nasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(unset)_                                           | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na maɓalli don danganta bayanan rajistar binciken MCP da asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(unset)_                                           | Legacy                                                      | Madadin tsohon suna na `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Yawan kwanakin da ake adana tarihin ayyukan A2A a ma’ajiyar bayanai ta cikin gida kafin sharewar yau da kullum ta goge layi. Idan ba a saita shi ba, ba lamba ba ne, ko kuma `<= 0`, ana komawa zuwa `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Maɓallin kashewa na fasalin lura da memory-hits na A2A. Saita shi zuwa `0` don tsallake neman tunawa daga ma’ajiyar ƙwaƙwalwa gaba ɗaya ga wani aiki; duk wani ƙima (har da wanda ba a saita ba) yana ci gaba da kunna shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana kunna ƙarshen API na Issue Agent na offline/local don yin triage na bayanan da aka yi rikodi. A bar shi a kashe sai idan ana gudanar da workflows na triage na bayanan da aka yi rikodi a local da gangan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(unset)_                                           | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) na gudun Issue Agent guda ɗaya na triage na bayanan da aka yi rikodi. Ana takaita shi zuwa matsakaicin ƙima na ciki; idan ba a saita shi ba ko kuma ba shi da inganci, ana komawa ga tsohuwar ƙimar da aka gina a ciki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(active context)_                                  | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Mahallin/profile na yanayin remote na CLI don umarnin `omniroute`; yana rinjayar mahallin da ke aiki a ma’ajiyar mahallin local. Ya yi daidai da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe backend na zaɓin keychain na tsarin aiki na `keytar` don bayanan shaidar mahallin CLI. Idan an kunna, bayanan shaidar suna nan a cikin yanayin `0600` na `config.json`, kuma CLI yana fitar da gargaɗin komawa sau ɗaya; an tsara shi ne don aiki na headless/container da aka yi da gangan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Tilasta sarrafa damar shiga bisa scope a kan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(all)_                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scopes da aka raba da waƙafi: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_RESEARCH_ENABLED`               | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/mcp-server/tools/perplexityResearch.ts`           | Yi rijistar kayan aikin MCP na Perplexity Deep Research. `1` kaɗai ne ke yin rijistar sa; duk wani abu dabam yana barin sa a kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Matse bayanan kayan aikin MCP kafin a mayar da manifest zuwa tsarin jerin bayanai. Ƙimomin da za su kunna shi: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algorithm/tsarin matse bayanai. Ƙimomin kashewa: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Iyakar lokacin dakatarwa (ms) don karatun gudanarwa na cikin MCP-server (lafiya, juriya, haɗe-haɗe, adadin amfani, amfani).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Iyakar lokacin dakatarwa (ms) don matakan MCP da suke jiran mai samarwa (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SESSION_IDLE_MS`                 | `300000`                                            | `open-sse/mcp-server/httpTransport.ts`                      | Rashin aiki TTL (ms) kafin sharewa ya girbi zaman zazzagewar MCP mara aiki. Ba a taɓa girbe zaman tare da buɗe rafi ko buƙatun cikin jirgin ba. Ƙimar da ba a saita ba, mara lamba, ko mara inganci suna komawa zuwa tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Matsakaicin adadin misalan fihirisar gida-corpus da aka adana a cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya (LRU, ɗaya a kowace jagorar tushen fihirisa). Manne zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Tazarar daidaita kundin samfura a cikin awoyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar binciken iyakar ƙima da adadin amfani na mai samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar (ms) tsakanin debo adadin OAuth a jere yayin daidaitawa mai yawa; ana debo haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don guje wa aika buƙatu da yawa lokaci guda zuwa upstream. `0` yana ficewa (na lokaci guda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) tsakanin debo adadin upstream a jere a hanyar preflight/monitor ta kowace buƙata; yana rarraba kiran hanyar sadarwa masu gudana lokaci guda domin asusu da yawa da ke kan IP ɗaya kada su aika buƙatu da yawa lokaci guda zuwa upstream. An haɗa shi da masu debo adadin Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (wuraren debo duka biyun), OpenCode, da Crof (#6009, #6911). Hanyar dispatch ta gama-gari `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/da sauransu) ba ta rufe tukuna — ana bibiyarta dabam. Ba ya shafar nasarorin cache. `0` yana kashe shi; ana iyakance shi zuwa `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Ƙarin tazarar bazuwar kowane buƙata (ms), ana zaɓarta a kowane karɓar keɓaɓɓen quota daga upstream. Tsoho shi ne 120; ana takaita shi zuwa `0..60000`; `0` yana cire jitter kaɗai. Saita `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` don kashe dukkan throttle. Jitter ba ya ba da tabbacin hana bans daga upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_QUOTA_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/codexQuotaFetcher.ts`                    | Ƙayyadaddun lokaci (ms) don ɗauko keɓaɓɓiyar keɓaɓɓiyar Codex guda ɗaya (`chatgpt.com/backend-api/wham/usage`). Jirgin yana jiran wannan ɗauko kafin aika buƙatar. Bayan ɓata lokaci ko kuskuren sufuri, za a sake amfani da hoton ƙarshe na mintuna 15 da suka gabata sai dai idan taga ta ƙare ko ta sake saitawa; ba tare da ko ɗaya ba, adadin ba a san shi ba, don haka an tsallake asusun bashi da aka biya don wannan buƙatar kuma ba a ci gaba da kwantar da hankali ba. Ya yarda da ingantacciyar lamba har zuwa `2147483647`; kowace ƙima ta koma ga tsoho.                                                                           |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Jinkiri (ms) kafin sabunta iyakokin provider bayan ainihin taron amfani, don ba API na quota na upstream lokaci ya yi rajistar amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | auto-ganewa                                         | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Cikakkar hanyar zuwa tsarin Chrome ko Edge wanda aka yi amfani da shi don ma'amala da Adobe Firefly shiga da sabuntawar kashe allo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | kunna                                               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Yana kiyaye IMS da jihar-hadarin burauza sabo tare da zaman Chrome CDP na asusu. Saita zuwa `0` don musaki sabuntawar burauza.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                    | kunna                                               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Ya dage da gyara zaman Adobe a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR` a duk lokacin sake farawa. Saita zuwa `0` don kiyaye ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya-kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`               | `12000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Mafi ƙarancin tazara a cikin milli seconds tsakanin Adobe Firefly yana haifar da ƙaddamarwa; `0` yana kashe tazara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Ƙarin lokacin shiru a cikin milli seconds bayan kowane nasara na uku na ƙaddamar da Adobe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Base backoff a millise seconds bayan raƙuman Adobe 408 na wucin gadi; haɗe tare da mika tazara a cikin mafi yawan ƙoƙari biyar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MEGABRAIN_API_URL`                             | `https://api.getmegabrain.com`                      | `open-sse/services/usage/megabrain.ts`                      | Tushen URL don zaman shirin MegaBrain da iyakokin mako-mako a Iyakokin Mai bayarwa (wanda aka inganta tare da alamar OAuth na haɗin ko maɓallin `mb-v1` API ); soke kawai don gudun ba da sanda ko na'urar gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Kashe duk sabis na baya: sabunta quota, daidaita iyakokin masu samarwa, dawo da haɗi, lafiyar bayanan shiga, daidaita cloud/model/free-proxy/proxy-health, share bayanan da suka wuce lokacin riƙewa, VACUUM da aka tsara, da binciken sake loda saituna ta atomatik. Yana da amfani ga CI/gwaji; a shigarwa mai daɗewa, haɗi ba sa dawo wa kai tsaye kuma bayanan ajiya na ci gaba da ƙaruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEADLESS`                            | _(unset)_                                           | `src/lib/system/headless.ts`                                | Fara injin proxy kawai: yana tsallake ƙananan tsarin zaɓi, `/dashboard` → 404. Daidai yake da `omniroute serve --headless`. Duba [HEADLESS.md](../guides/HEADLESS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_RUNTIME_BACKGROUND_TASKS`     | _(unset)_                                           | `src/lib/config/runtimeSettings.ts`                         | Tilasta kunna ayyukan baya yayin gano gwaji na atomatik. Saita `1` don yin watsi da hasashen gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUDGET_RESET_JOB_INTERVAL_MS`        | `600000`                                            | `src/lib/jobs/budgetResetJob.ts`                            | Mitar duba sake saita kasafi (ms). Mafi ƙarancin daraja `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`     | `60000`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Mitar dawo da haɗi daga yanayin sanyaya cikin himma (ms): tana sake tabbatar da haɗin da `rate_limited_until` na wucin gadi ya ƙare, ba tare da sanya hakan a hanyar sarrafa buƙata mai zafi ba. Mafi ƙarancin daraja `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY`         | `false`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Kashe mai tsara dawo da haɗi daga yanayin sanyaya cikin himma (dawo da haɗi a hankali a cikin `getProviderCredentials` har yanzu yana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_CACHE_CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000`                                           | `src/lib/jobs/reasoningCacheCleanupJob.ts`                  | Mitar share ma’ajiyar reasoning (ms). | Variable            | Default     | Source File                     | Description                                        |
| ------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | auto-detect | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL ɗin sabar don gadar tantancewar CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token ɗin tantancewa don gadar CLI.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`       | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID ɗin mai amfani don zaman gadar CLI.             |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin tsohon tsarin na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.       |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin tsohon tsarin na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.        |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(unset)_   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin tsohon tsarin na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.      |

## 11. OAuth Takardun Mai bayarwa

### Sabunta alamar Codex mai wucewa

Saitin `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN_REFRESH_PASSIVE_PROVIDERS` zuwa `codex` yana sanya wannan misalin ya zama mabukaci na daidaitattun takaddun shaidar Codex, ba mai jujjuyawar alama ba. Tsohuwar fanko ko rashin saiti tana kiyaye yanayin wartsakewa na yau da kullun. An gyara dabi'un da aka raba waƙafi kuma ba su da hankali; `cx` an lakafta shi ne ga `codex`. Masu ba da tallafi sun gaza tabbatar da farawa. Sauran masu samarwa ba su canzawa.

A wuraren wartsakewa da ake dasu, misalin yana ƙetare haɗin karanta cache kuma yana ɗaukar wata alama ta daban, mara ƙarewa. Alamu da aka ƙi a sama yayin buƙatun ba za su iya sake cancanta ba bayan aiki tare sun koma baya: buƙatun buƙatun suna riƙe a mafi yawan hotunan yatsu SHA-256 guda huɗu, an jefar da su tare da wannan buƙatar. Filayen alamar kawai aka karɓa, don haka metadata mai bada buƙatu na gida ya tsira. Babu wasu takaddun shaida da aka dage. Idan babu musanya, `refresh_disabled` ba ta ƙare ba ce kuma baya ƙidaya zuwa ga mai wartsakewa mai wartsakewa. Amsoshin 401/403 na ainihi na ainihi suna riƙe lissafin gazawar al'ada, sanyi mai ma'ana da kurakuran abokin ciniki; Har yanzu siginar kashe asusu na kulle har abada.

Wartsakewa dashboard/API na hannu yana dawo da `409` tare da lambar `refresh_disabled` da umarni don wartsake akan misalin aiki da aiki tare. Sabuntawa a kaikaice (tattaunawa, amfani, duba lafiyar lafiya da tabbatar da shigo da kaya) baya bayyana wannan kuskuren; amfani da ake buƙata 401 maidowa yana sake gwadawa sau ɗaya tare da abin da aka ɗauka tare da aiki tare. shigo da Codex auth yana adana alamun da aka kawo ba tare da musanya mai wartsakewa ba; sabbin mashigai OAuth ba su canzawa. Ba a ƙara aiki tare kafin aika aiki a waje da hanyar wartsakewa da ke akwai.

Ajiye daidai mai jujjuya guda ɗaya kuma shirya aikin aiki tare daban, kafin alamun samun damar ƙarewa (tsohon jagorar jagora: mintuna biyar). Wannan zaɓin ba kulle ba ne da aka rarraba, kwafin bayanai ko jigilar SQLite da aka raba ba. Source: `open-sse/services/tokenRefresh/passiveCodex.ts`.

Gina-in takardun shaida don **ci gaban localhost** . Don ƙaddamar da aiki mai nisa, yi rijistar naku a kowane na'ura mai haɓakawa na mai samarwa.

| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                      | Wurin aiki ID don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                      | Alias ga `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                      | Share Qoder CLI config dir (zamanin PAT keɓe, yana guje wa shigar da mai bincike).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                      | Matsakaicin matakan qodercli na lokaci ɗaya (buguwar bugu ko tsokanar ACP ); karin buƙatun layi FIFO. Unset (tsoho) = babu hula.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                      | Yaya tsawon lokacin da buƙatar Qoder ke jiran ramin qodercli (kawai tare da saitin hula) kafin kasawa da 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. Default `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                      | `print` (tsoho) spawns `qodercli --print` kowace bukata; `acp` yana kiyaye tsari guda ɗaya `qodercli --acp` a kowane PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                      | safarar ACP : kashe tsarin qodercli na ci gaba bayan wannan lokacin rashin aiki. Default `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                      | safarar ACP : ranar ƙarshe don qodercli don amsa ACP `initialize`. Tsohuwar `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                      | safarar ACP : sake sarrafa tsarin qodercli na dindindin bayan wannan zaman da yawa. Default `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web               | Alamar `tk` ta gaba don aikawa azaman `validated` akan `/api/chat`. Ana buƙatar lokacin da Blackbox ya tilasta madaidaicin alamar; in ba haka ba OmniRoute ya koma zuwa ga bazuwar UUID. Duba fitowar #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge guardrail    | BudeAI-mai jituwa tushen URL don kiran gada mara hangen nesa na Anthropic. Tsoffin abubuwan gadar OpenAI URL env ko api.openai.com. Nuna madauki na OmniRoute's `/v1` kai ko kowane madaidaicin buɗaɗɗen buɗaɗɗen AI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Mas'ala ta #2232. Lokacin da URL ya zama OmniRoute `/v1` kansa, ƙaramar buƙatar siffanta tana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma ta inganta tare da ƙudurin shaidar madauki (`sk_omniroute` sentinel a yanayin gida, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) don haka `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ƙaddamar da aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge guardrail    | maɓallin API don URL na sama. Yana soke kowane mai bayarwa OpenAI / Google env vars don kiran gadar hangen nesa ba na ɗan adam ba. Samfuran Anthropic suna kiyaye hanyar maɓalli Anthropic. Mas'ala ta #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge guardrail    | Yaya tsawon lokacin da "babu ɗan takarar da za a iya amfani da shi" yana ɓoye sakamakon karkatar, a cikin ms (tsoho `30000`). Ƙididdiga marasa inganci ko mara kyau sun koma ga tsoho; `0` yana kashe ma'ajin mara kyau. Source: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_IMAGE_NORMALIZATION`             | Daidaita hoton hangen nesa | Shiga (cire = kashe, canjin hali sifili; `1`/`true`/`on` yana kunna). Yana daidaita kowane hoto na layi (base64 data URI / Claude `source.data`) a cikin jikin buƙatun hira kafin aikawa: Ana amfani da daidaitawar EXIF, RGB (alpha flattened on white), dogon gefen `<= 4096` px, jimlar pixels `<= ~4M`; Hotunan da ke buƙatar canji ana sake shigar da su azaman tushe JPEG ( PNG maras alpha-free PNGs zama marar asara), RGB JPEG/PNG da ya rigaya ya bi ta hanyar da ba a taɓa shi ba. Ana gyara kasafin kuɗi. Nuna bututun hangen nesa Qwen2-VL-style waɗanda ke ƙin hotunan oversized/exotic tare da kurakurai 400 ko rataye fitar da sifili. Ba a sauke URLs http(s) mai nisa ba; binary `sharp` da ya ɓace ko hoton da ba a iya yankewa ya wuce ta wanda ba a taɓa shi ba (fail-bude). Maimaita babban hoto yana ƙara jinkirin buƙata. Source: `open-sse/utils/visionImageNormalization.ts`. |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Takaddun shaida](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri OAuth 2.0 Client ID (nau'in: "Aikace-aikacen Yanar Gizo")
> 3. Ƙara URL uwar garken ku azaman Mai ba da izini ga URI
> 4. Maye gurbin ƙimar ƙimar a cikin `.env`.

## 12. Wakilin Mai Amfani-Aiki Ya Hana

Soke taken `User-Agent` da aka aika zuwa kowane mai samarwa. Ana warware wannan da ƙarfi a lokacin aiki ta rukunin tushe mai zartarwa:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Source:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Mai canzawa | Default Value | Lokacin Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ta fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don tura sunayen kayan aikin kayan aiki na ɓangare na uku kai tsaye zuwa Anthropic akan duka hanyoyin da ke ɗaure ɗan adam ( OAuth na ƙasa da CLIProxyAPI). Ta hanyar tsohuwa mai zartarwa yana ƙididdige sunayen kayan aikin da ba Claude-Code ba (Claude Code taswirar taswirar inda akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase) kuma ya juya su akan amsa ta hanyar `_toolNameMap`, don haka ba a ƙi yin amfani da kayan aikin maciji ba kamar yadda abokan ciniki na ɓangare na uku suka buga. Gyara kurakurai kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ke sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Soke sigar abokin ciniki Codex ba tare da cikakken zaren UA ba |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Haɓaka `x-grok-client-version` don ƙididdigewa, ƙirar ƙirar aiki, da lambar na'ura OAuth, da tsohowar Grok CLI Mai amfani-Agent don ƙaddamarwa da daidaitawa samfuri. Yana karɓar SemVer tare da metadata na zaɓin prerelease/build, har zuwa haruffa 128 bayan datsa kewayen sararin samaniya. Cire, fanko, mara inganci, ko ƙimar da ke ƙunshe da layin suna amfani da fil ɗin da aka aika. Cikakken bayanin mai amfani-Agent mai amfani yana riƙe da fifiko. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Soke sigar lambar lambar Claude da aka tallata ba tare da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic yana ba da wasu samfura akan wannan ƙimar (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Soke karin haruffa 3 OmniRoute da ke rataye zuwa `cc_version=` a cikin toshe lissafin Claude. Yi karo tare da `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — liƙa sigar kawai tana tallata `version.revision` biyu babu ainihin binary wanda ke fitarwa. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Soke sigar Copilot CLI da aka tallata ba tare da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya sabunta |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_CL` | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | Antigravity CLI canji na Piper da aka ruwaito a `cl=` `agy` cikin CLI -Agent-Agent. Ba za a iya samo shi daga sigar ba kuma ba a fallasa ta jama'a ba; iri a kowane sigar daga kamawa kai tsaye (1.4.4 → `1003595037`). Cirewa akan wani sigar da ba a sani ba yana barin `cl=` maimakon tallan lissafin canji na wani gini. |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin sabunta Kiro IDE |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Soke Kiro social device-code `clientId` (jama'a id) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Ficewa: cikakken saƙon kowane-firam ingantacciyar CRC akan rafin taron Kiro (gyara gurbatattun rafukan) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD da Kiro credit; yana canza kiredit `meteringEvent` zuwa ainihin farashin buƙatu |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Rasa martanin Kiro wanda ke ba da rahoton rashin kiredit maimakon yin rikodin shi ba a biya shi ba |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin sabunta Qoder CLI |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ta sabunta |

> [!TIP]
> Kuna iya ƙara wakiltan mai amfani don** kowane** mai bada ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Mai zartarwa yana gina sunan env var a hankali.

## 13. CLI Daidaituwar Sawun yatsa

Lokacin da aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake yin odar kanun HTTP da filayen jikin JSON don dacewa da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yin alamar asusu yayin da ake adana IP wakilin ku.

**Source:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Mai bayarwa

| Mai canzawa              | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                      |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Mimics Codex CLI buƙatun sa hannu           |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Mimics Claude Code sa hannun neman sa hannu |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Mimics GitHub Copilot buƙatun sa hannu      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Mimics Antigravity buƙatar sa hannu         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Mimics Cursor buƙatar sa hannu              |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Mimics Kimi Coding sa hannun neman sa hannu |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Mimics Kilo Code sa hannu                   |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Mimics Cline buƙatar sa hannu               |

### Duniya

| Mai canzawa      | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna dacewa da hoton yatsa don** duka** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Kimi Coding CLI ainihi ya soke

| Mai canzawa             | Tsohuwar           | Fayil na asali                           | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ------------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`           | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Soke sigar Kimi CLI da aka aika yayin kiran OAuth/API.                                |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(wanda aka kama)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Soke na'urar Kimi da aka kama ID da aka yi amfani da ita a cikin taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sokewar Wakilin Mai amfani (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana ɗaukar odar kan kai da odar filin jiki, yayin da Wakilin Mai amfani ya soke takamammen takamaiman UA. Ana iya kunna duka biyun kai tsaye.
