## 14. API Mabudin Masu Bayarwa

maɓallan API don masu samarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye.** Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Masu bayarwa → Ƙara maɓallin API.

Saita ta hanyar masu canjin yanayi madadin Docker ko turawa mara kai.

Samfurin da aka gane: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mai canzawa        | Mai bayarwa                                    |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                       |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                     |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader fallback)     |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (alias for `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)          |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) saka + chat fallback |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (alias for `GEMINI_API_KEY`)            |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` a tsaye don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Rikici, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a cikin v3.8.0 saboda lokacin gudu ba ya karanta su — waɗannan masu samarwa sun dogara kawai akan Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / rufaffen DB. Dubi sashin _Audit: Cire / Matattu Matattu a kasan wannan takaddar don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su a cikin SQLite kuma suna fifiko akan masu canjin yanayi.
>
> **Jina:** saka `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` yi** ba** lissafin gungu env key lokacin da dashboard `jina-ai` (ko shared `jina-reader`) dangane ya wanzu — `getProviderCredentials` ne cika-farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` kawai lokacin da babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Lokutan kira suna danganta faɗuwar env azaman `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Karatu (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) baya hidimar `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (alias `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana amfani da haɗin dashboard `gemini` da farko. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` kawai lokacin da babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Maganganun kira suna danganta faɗuwar env azaman `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Hanyoyin zirga-zirgar ababen hawa na asali suna amfani da `x-goog-api-key` akan `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — N OpenAI `input` abubuwa sun zama N vectors.

---

## 15. Saitunan Lokacin Ƙarewa

Dukkan ƙimomi suna cikin **milisecond**. Ana warware su a wuri guda a cikin `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsawon Lokaci

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (global override)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (upstream provider calls, default: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (independent, default: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (independent, default: 0 = off, suggested: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (independent, default: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent; unset: 1260000, raised by declared budgets; set: hard cap; 0 disables)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (caps adaptive readiness extensions, default: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (derived, default: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 0 = disabled)
```

| Maɓalli                                           | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | _(unset)_                                                         | Gajeriyar hanya ta duniya — tana rinjayar tsoffin ƙimomin `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar HTTP ga kiran mai samarwa na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin shiru tsakanin ɗanyen bytes na sama kafin a soke aikin. Samfuran tunani mai zurfi ba safai suke yin tsayawa sama da 90s ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin SSE mai aiki; ba ya sake farawa idan an karɓi bytes daga sama, kuma ya kasance mai zaman kansa daga `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, tsohon ƙimar ita ce mafi girman `timeoutMs` na kowane samfuri a cikin rajista (1200000, Codex) tare da ƙarin 60000, kuma target da ya ayyana kasafin lokaci mafi tsawo (connection `timeoutMs`, sannan na kowane samfuri, sannan na matakin mai samarwa, misali GLM's 3000000) zai samu wannan kasafin tare da ƙarin 60000. Ƙima mai kyau ita ce iyaka mai tsauri ga kowane target da kasafin da aka ayyana ba za su iya ƙara ba. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe shi ga kowane target.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                          | _(disabled)_                                                      | Ko OmniRoute zai iya fitar da layukan sharhi na SSE `:` (misali bugun zuciyar `: keepalive` da ƙarin bayanan metadata na `x-omniroute-*`). An kashe shi ta tsohuwa (#10524) saboda abokan cinikin da suka dace da OpenAI kuma suke da tsauraran ƙa'idoji suna yin JSON.parse ga kowace layin SSE kuma su faɗi saboda sharhin `:`; bugun zuciyar `data:` bai shafe shi ba. Saita zuwa `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake kunna shi. Ana amfani da shi ta `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                                 | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Zaɓi ne: haɗa abubuwan `content_block_delta` na Claude masu zuwa jere (mai fihirisar toshe da nau’in delta iri ɗaya) da ake aika wa abokan cinikin Claude, tare da aika su aƙalla kowane N ms. Ba ya rasa bayanai; ba a taɓa haɗa `signature_delta` da abubuwan kan iyaka ba. Yana aiki ga kowane nau’in delta sai dai idan an saita mabambantan canji na kowane nau’i a ƙasa. `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                        | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Sake tsara ƙima ta kowane nau’i ta `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                            | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Sake tsara ƙima ta kowane nau’i ta `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                      | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Sake tsara ƙima ta kowane nau’i ta `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                           | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Zaɓi ne: tattara firam ɗin SSE da aka tura cikin N ms zuwa rubutu guda da ake aika wa abokin ciniki. Ba a taɓa jinkirta firam na farko da firam na ƙarshe (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) ba; idan an kunna tattarawa, ITL yana auna gibin tsakanin tarin firam. `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                              | `timer`                                                           | Aiwatar da mai sa ido kan tsayawar baiti don amsoshin da ake watsawa. `timer` yana sake kunna mai ƙidayar lokaci ɗaya ga kowane gutsuren bayanai daga sama (yana aiki daidai lokacin da kasafin tsayawa ya cika); `interval` yana riƙe da bincike guda ɗaya ga kowane rafi tare da tazarar `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, don haka tsayawa zai faru cikin kasafin tsayawa + tazarar bincike. `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin karɓar taron SSE na farko wanda ba ping ba. | `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran shirye-shiryen farkon abu don tafiyar da agent ɗin `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta musu ta `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Wannan na iya ƘARA dawo da yawo ne kawai a kan tsoffin saitunan mai gudanarwa — ba ya taɓa rinjayar zaɓin kashewa na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB da aka fayyace kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`         | `true`                                                            | Cire abubuwan SSE marasa bin ƙa’ida na `codex.*` (misali, `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke jawo `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK ya dawo da 502. Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                    | _(unset)_                                                         | Zaɓin jigilar Codex ta app-server. Ƙarshen WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) na wani `codex app-server` na gefe na gida. Idan an saita shi tare da alama, ana tura buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa app-server na gefe maimakon HTTP Responses API. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ga kowace haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`              | _(unset)_                                                         | Alamar izini/bearer da aka saka kai tsaye kuma ake gabatarwa ga app-server. Sauyawa ga kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`         | _(unset)_                                                         | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin da ke riƙe da token na damar app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi lokacin da ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`               | _(unset)_                                                         | Manufar sandbox da ake miƙawa ga juyawar app-server (misali, `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — ana ƙin tambayoyin ta atomatik; kiran kayan aikin harness bai shafi wannan ba (suna bi ta hanyar `item/tool/call` ta daban). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar kanun amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`            | `110000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jira kanun amsa a kan buƙatun streaming (ms). `0` yana kashe wannan iyaka; `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na mai bayarwa da aka ayyana yana da fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_IMAGE_RESPONSE_START_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000` (minti 10)                                               | Ranar ƙarshe na farawa na amsawa-farko (ms) don biya Codex image-generation/edit POSTs, duka kai tsaye kuma ta hanyar wakili na HTTP(S)/SOCKS. Ba a taɓa sake kunna waɗannan buƙatun ba bayan shuɗewar lokaci mai ma'ana ko gazawar hanyar sadarwa. Relays na Edge (`vercel`/`deno`/`cloudflare`) kuma suna yin ƙoƙari ɗaya, amma kiyaye nasu `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ranar ƙarshe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowace ƙoƙarin kai tsaye ba tare da proxy ba. Idan lokacin ya ƙare, za a sake gwadawa sau ɗaya ta sabuwar socket; saita `0` don kashe iyakar kuma a riƙe halayen da suka gabata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `300000` (5 min)                                                  | Ƙaramin lokacin fara amsa (ms) ga ƙoƙarin kai tsaye ba tare da proxy ba wanda host ɗin da ake nufi yake na private/LAN/loopback (misali `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), domin kada cold start na Ollama na asali ya katse saboda tsohuwar 30s da ke sama. Yana aiki a matsayin iyaka ta ƙasa: lokacin jiran da ake amfani da shi a zahiri shi ne mafi girma tsakanin wannan ƙima da `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ko ƙimar da ba ta inganta ba yana cire iyakar ƙasar ta local. Ba ya shafar remote hosts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_VOLUME_ADAPTIVE`        | `1`                                                               | Ƙara lokacin fara amsa kai tsaye gwargwadon matakin girman bayanan buƙata: jikkuna da suka fi 512 KB suna samun ×2, waɗanda suka fi 2 MB suna samun ×3, tare da iyaka a 180 s, kuma ba ya rage wata iyaka ta ƙasa da ta riga ta fi girma (override na operator ko iyakar local/LAN da ke sama). Manyan contexts suna yin prefill daidai gwargwadon girman shigarwa ba tare da reasoning selector da zai dogara da shi wajen saita iyakar reasoning ba, don haka taga madaidaiciya tana katse upstreams masu jinkirin prefill masu lafiya daidai a iyakar. Saita `0`/`false`/`off` don kulle halayen madaidaiciya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`      | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Iyakar sama (ms) ga ƙoƙarin RETRY ta sabuwar socket bayan lokacin fara amsar ƙoƙarin pooled ya ƙare a sama (#13703). Yana aiki ne kawai idan mai kira ya riga ya haɗa siginar wa’adinsa (cascade na resolved connection/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`); wannan siginar ita ce ainihin iyaka kuma tana aiki da farko a hanyar da aka nufa, don haka wannan madadin kariya ce mai faɗi maimakon madaidaicin cap — ba tare da shi ba, retry ɗin zai sake amfani da taga gajere iri ɗaya ta `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` kamar ƙoƙarin pooled, kuma ya ba da 504 ga reasoning models masu lafiya amma masu jinkirin TTFB. Ba a taɓa barin sa ya kasance ƙasa da iyakar madaidaiciya da ke sama ba; idan mai kira bai samar da siginar deadline ba kwata-kwata, retry ɗin yana riƙe da iyakar madaidaiciya ba tare da canji ba. |

Lokacin ƙarewa yana soke mai karanta wreq sannan ya koma ga mai rarraba kai tsaye/wakili; `0` yana kashe wannan watchdog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `15000`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai, kuma sai idan tutar fasalin `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` tana kunne (kashe ta ne a tsohuwa): yana iyakance jiran byte na farko na jikin amsar Responses da ake watsawa bayan kanun bayanai (#13484). Rafin Responses yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru bayan wannan tazara alama ce ta tsayawa: ana sanyaya asusun kuma buƙatar tana komawa zuwa asusu na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa ta biyu tana gaza nan take. `0` yana kashe kariyar ko da tutar tana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`              | `0` (= kashe)                                                     | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai: yana iyakance jiran kanun amsar upstream a kiran Responses da ake watsawa yayin da har yanzu akwai wani asusu — buƙatar da aka jera tana daina jira ta wuce gaba maimakon ta riƙe cikakken lokacin jiran kanun bayanai. Kiran Responses da ake watsawa ne kaɗai abin ya shafa (rafin Responses yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru a can jerin gwiwa ne, ba samarwa ba); kammalawar hira da kiran da ba a watsa ba ba su taɓu ba, kuma wannan tazara tana rage iyakar fara fetch mai tasiri ne kawai, ba ta ƙara shi ba. Ƙimar da aka ba da shawara yayin kunnawa: `30000`. `0` yana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Asusu na ƙarshe da ya rage koyaushe yana riƙe cikakken lokacin jiran kanun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                        | `false`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai: ajiye buƙatar tare da heartbeat bayan 429 na wucin gadi da yawa (ko sabon alamar matsin tafkin), sannan a sake kunna wani zagaye guda mai iyaka wanda ya ƙunshi har zuwa asusu 3 a jere maimakon a rarraba ta ga dukkan rundunar (#13924). | `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`             | `false`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai: idan duk asusun da lokacin sanyayarsu ya ƙare a zagayen sake kunnawa na ajiye-da-ci-gaba an ware su saboda ƙwaƙwalwar ƙin amincewar wakili, a yi musu hidima duk da haka maimakon a sake kunna komai (#14851, ci gaba ne na #14750). A kashe yake a tsohuwa: zagayen sake kunnawa ba ya ƙunsar komai a wannan yanayin, kamar yadda yake kafin #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                    | `false`                                                           | Don tattaunawar yawo kawai: idan jikin upstream na farko ya tsaya kafin ya samar da wani lamari mai amfani, a yi ƙoƙari na biyu mai iyaka ta hanyar routing iri ɗaya, da kasafin shirye-shirye iri ɗaya, ba tare da hukunta asusu ba. | `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                          | `false`                                                           | Don mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: bayan 429 daga mai bayarwa da aka rarraba bisa guga na egress a kan asusun da ba shi da proxy a cikin mahallin pool na yanzu, a nemi wani memba daga pool ɗin haɗi don ƙoƙari na gaba maimakon sake gwada adireshin egress ɗin nan. Yana tsara fifiko, ba ya warewa: pool da ya ƙare yana ci gaba da halayen yanzu. An kashe shi ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana juya zuwa asusu na gaba kamar yadda yake a da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `60000`                                                           | Ƙayyadadden lokacin jiran buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                     | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin sassaucin wa'adin ƙarshe na JS gaba ɗaya, wanda ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin jiran na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Ƙayyadadden lokacin jiran buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` yana ƙara shi zuwa `180000` ga kowace buƙata don samarwa masu tsawo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin sassaucin wa'adin ƙarshe na JS gaba ɗaya, wanda ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin jiran na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                          | `on`                                                              | Rukunin burauzar Playwright da aka raba don tattaunawar kukin yanar gizo da burauza ke tallafawa (`browserPool.ts`); saita shi zuwa `off` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                     | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanyar zuwa binary na `obscura` da ake amfani da shi a matsayin injin farko ta rukunin burauzar da mai aiwatar da Cloudflare Playground (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); ana gano shi ta atomatik daga tsarin PATH idan ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                            | _(unset)_                                                         | Nuna wani Obscura da ke aiki tuni (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da wani; tsarin ba shi ne ke kula da wannan aikin ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                    | `random free port`                                                | Takamaiman tashar jiragen ruwa don `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar tashar da ba ta aiki ta atomatik idan ba a saita ta ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                          | `0`                                                               | Zaɓi buƙatar tattaunawar kuki ta yanar gizo ta bi hanyar da ke amfani da burauza (`browserBackedChat.ts`); saita zuwa `1` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                               | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | Tushen URL na mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (Connect-RPC na kimi.ai na ƙasa da ƙasa) (`kimi-web.ts`); a canza shi kawai don wuraren madubi/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                               | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken ƙarshen hanyar tattaunawa na mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                    | _(an gano ta atomatik)_                                           | Hanyar zuwa tsarin Chrome/Edge wanda za'a iya aiwatarwa don shigar da Adobe Firefly m browser (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); ya soke kowane-OS auto-ganewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                        | _.build/madaidaicin fitarwa_                                      | Ƙaddamar da lokacin ginawa don jagorar fitarwa na tsaye wanda aka cinye ta hanyar haɗin ginin bayan ginin (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); gina kayan aiki, ba lokacin aiki ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Ƙoƙarin haɗaka na haɗakarwa sun gaji ƙarewar lokacin buƙatun da aka warware (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` lokacin da yake ba da tsohowar debo). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin haɗaɗɗen haɗin kai, rashin daidaituwa, ko mai badawa ya soke kawai don yin haɗin gwiwa cikin sauri; dabi'un da ke sama da lokacin da aka yi sama na yanzu an keɓe su zuwa lokacin ƙarewar sama.

`comboTimeoutMs` shine keɓantaccen kasafin kuɗi na agogon bango gabaɗaya a cikin maƙasudan gazawar. Bar shi ba a saita shi ba ko `0` don ci gaba da jujjuyawar mara iyaka (tsayawa mai tsauri na minti 10 a cikin `comboPredicates.ts` har yanzu yana aiki). Ƙimar da ta dace ta maye gurbin waccan hanyar aminci don haɗawa. Riƙe `comboTimeoutMs` fiye da `targetTimeoutMs` don haka gazawar har yanzu yana da lokaci bayan jinkirin manufa ta farko.

### Matsakaicin Mai Sake Wuta

Tunatar da matakin mai ba da wutar lantarki. Tsoffin suna nuna ma'auni masu ƙima da aka yi amfani da su tun v3.6 don haɗin + 500.

| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Lokacin sake saitawa (ms) na breaker na mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Lokacin sake saitawa (ms) na breaker na mai samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da gida (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Lokacin sake saitawa (ms) na breaker na mai samar da gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Breaker na matakin mai samarwa: adadin gazawa a cikin lokacin kafin dukan mai samar da OAuth ya shiga lokacin dakatarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Matsakaicin mai ninkawa na ƙarin resetTimeout na mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Breaker na matakin mai samarwa: lokacin ƙidayar gazawa mai zamewa (ms) ga masu samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: tazarar lissafin gazawa mai zungurawa (ms) ga masu samarwa na cikin gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Ƙididdiga marasa inganci ko marasa ƙididdiga sun koma ga tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Sake saitin sama mai tsayi koyaushe yana samun nasara. Ƙididdiga marasa inganci ko marasa ƙididdiga sun koma ga tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Matakin ja da baya da ake ɗaukar mai samarwa na pin ɗin ajiyar mahallin a matsayin mai rashin lafiya na dindindin, sannan a cire pin ɗin domin canzawa zuwa wani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Tazarar hana sauyawa ba da gaira ba (ms) da ke jure gajerun lokutan dakatarwa na wucin gadi kafin a cire pin ɗin ajiyar mahallin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                            | Tsarawa                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Samar da lambar da ke ɗaukar lokaci mai tsawo** | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (minti 15)                             |
| **Ƙayyade jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin**         | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (minti 21)                      |
| **Gaggauta kasa ga API na samarwa**               | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                                |
| **Samfuran tunani mai tsawo**                     | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (minti 5 tsakanin gutsure-gutsure) |

## 16. Rajista

Tsarin rajista yana rubutawa zuwa stdout da kuma fayilolin rajista masu juyawa. Ana karanta dukkan saituna daga `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Sauyi                                     | Tsoho                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Mafi ƙarancin matakin rajista: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Tsarin fitarwa: `text` (mai sauƙin karantawa ga mutum) ko `json` (mai tsari).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Rubuta rajista zuwa fayil tare da stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Hanyar fayil ɗin rajista (dangane da tushen aikin ko `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Matsakaicin girman fayil kafin juyawa. Yana karɓar: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ko bayit kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana fayilolin rajistar manhaja da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Matsakaicin adadin kwafin ajiyar fayilolin rajista da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana shigarwar rajistar buƙata/kira a cikin rumbun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar rajistar kira a cikin ma'ajiyar ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `call_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_          | Tilasta kunna ko kashe cikakken rajistar buƙata, tare da ƙetare saitin dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (awa 1)          | Matsakaicin shekarun shigarwar rajistar buƙata mai aiki da ta zama marar mai ita kafin tsaftace ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MAX_PENDING_DETAILS`                     | `5000`                     | Matsakaicin cikakkun bayanai masu jiran aiki (cikakkiyar lamba fiye da sifili); iyakar adadi: yayin sharewa; iyakokin byte/map: yayin sakawa.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Adana gutsattsarin kwarara a cikin kayayyakin pipeline lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Dole ne a zaɓi kunnawa (`true`) — a kashe yake ta tsohuwa don adana sararin faifai.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Matsakaicin girman kayan rajistar kiran pipeline a KB lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `proxy_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/fita da farkon asusu a cikin rajistocin console na `[ProxyEgress]`. Bayanan rajistar proxy na dashboard/rumbun bayanai suna riƙe da cikakkun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don yin rikodin `first_chunk_ms` (daga fara aikawa zuwa bayit na farko mai amfani na jiki) a layukan rajistar proxy. Yana naɗe kowane jikin upstream a cikin lazy passthrough stream sannan ya gyara layin da zarar bayit ɗin ya iso; a kashe yake ta tsohuwa domin martani su wuce ba tare da an taɓa su ba. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (minti 1)          | Sau nawa `src/lib/logRotation.ts` ke sake duba girman fayil ɗin rajista mai aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Matsakaicin tsawon kirtani da ake riƙewa a cikin kayayyakin rajistar hira (tsoho 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Adadin abubuwan jeri da ake riƙewa daga ƙarshen jeri lokacin gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Matsakaicin zurfin kafa-cikin-kafa kafin a gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Matsakaicin adadin maɓallan object da ake riƙewa a cikin payload na rajistar hira (0 = babu iyaka).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Girman dukkan jikin buƙata/martani (KB) kafin a maye gurbinsa da taƙaitaccen bayani kawai maimakon cikakken kwafi. Ƙara wannan idan dogayen tattaunawar wakili suna nuna alamar riƙe wuri maimakon ainihin saƙonni a dashboard.                                                                                                            |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Lokacin da yake true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` yana tsallake gajartawa bisa girma. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
