## 17. Ƙwaƙwalwar ƙwaƙwalwa

| Mai canzawa                                  | Tsohuwar                                                                                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEAP_DIAGNOSTICS_ENABLED`         | `0` (kashe)                                                                                         | Saita `1` don shigar da ma'aunin ma'aunin matsi na V8 daga `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`. Girman rajistan ayyukan da ƙidayar tashin jirgin sama, kar a taɓa buƙatar abun ciki, burbushi ko ɗimbin hotuna. Yana gudana a cikin na'urar rikodi-madauki, don haka `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS=0` kuma tana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEAP_DIAGNOSTICS_THRESHOLD_MB`    | `900`                                                                                               | Madaidaicin madaidaicin kofa don heapUsed (MiB) wanda ke haifar da rajistan shiga bincike. Ƙimar da ba ta aiki ba suna amfani da 900 MiB; Ana iyakance saƙonni masu tarin yawa zuwa sau ɗaya a cikin daƙiƙa 60.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`                        | _auto_ (karfe bare);**`1024` a cikin hoton Docker**                                                 | **Nasiha** Docker/standalone V8 heap iyaka (MB). Lokacin da ba a saita ba, an daidaita shi da ƙarfi (~ 35% na tsarin RAM, manne zuwa `[512, 4096]`); `512` shine kawai kasa lokacin da jimlar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya ba za a iya karantawa ba. A kan `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), an** bayyane** an haɗa darajar kamar `--max-old-space-size` da** yayi nasara** sama da tuta tuta mai karo da NODE_OPTIONS (tuta ta ƙarshe ta V8). har yanzu `omniroute serve` ya fi son tulin NODE_OPTIONS data kasance (#5238). Kar a saita duka biyu zuwa lambobi daban-daban — tsarin yana rubuta gargaɗin suna duka ƙima da mai nasara.** Hoton Docker na hukuma koyaushe yana saita `1024`, don haka daidaitawa baya gudana a wurin.** Wakilin coding `/v1/responses` yana buƙatar `8192`–`12288` tare da babban dakin taro — duba [Docker Jagorar — lokacin gudu RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`                  | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; kashe lokacin da ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ba) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Cikakkun tsari - fuse RSS (MB) don ƙwaƙwalwar native/external wanda V8 `heapUsed` ba zai iya gani ba; sama da shi sababbin buƙatun samun 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS ba kasafai yake faduwa ba bayan karu, don haka hada shi da `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                      | `50`                                                                                                | Matsakaicin cache tsarin shigar da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                                    | Matsakaicin jimlar girman cache mai sauri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`                        | `300000` (5 min)                                                                                    | Shigar cache da sauri TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                    | `100`                                                                                               | Matsakaicin cache zazzabi = 0 martani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                   | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                                    | Matsakaicin girman ma'aunin ma'auni.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`                      | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                                  | Semantic cache shigarwar TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                                  | SQLite datsa tazara. `0` yana hana farawa da gudana na lokaci-lokaci; sauran dabi'u da ke ƙasa `60000` sun koma ga tsoho. Ƙimar da ke sama da `2147483647` ms suna matsawa zuwa matsakaicin jinkirin lokacin Node, guje wa ambaliya zuwa jefa ƙuri'a 1ms. Na farko gudu bayan 1s; masu ƙidayar lokaci ba su da ƙarfi kuma ba su taɓa haɗuwa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_BATCH_SIZE`  | `2000`                                                                                              | Layukan kowace SQLite datsa DELETE, manne zuwa `[100, 50000]`; yana haifar da madauki tsakanin batches.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_MAX_BATCHES` | `50`                                                                                                | Rarraba kasafin batch DELETE a kowace gudu a kan iyakacin ƙarewar ƙarewa da na zaɓin wuce iyaka, manne zuwa `[1, 10000]`. Ana ci gaba da rikodin bayanan baya akan abubuwan da ke gaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_ROWS`          | `0`                                                                                                 | Zabin SQLite hular jere (`0` = babu). Bayan share `expires_at <=` tambarin lokaci-farawa ISO, fitar da `created_at` mafi tsufa, sannan `rowid`, gami da layuka masu rai, dangane da tsarin kasafin kuɗi da aka raba. An yanke ƙidayar zuwa lamba (har zuwa iyakar amintaccen lamba). Dabbobi, marasa adadi, marasa iyaka ko munanan dabi'u ga duk masu canjin prune guda huɗu sun koma ga gazawarsu; ɓangarorin zagaye ƙasa. Yanke baya canza TTL, memory LRU ko yanayin cache vector.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                | `5`                                                                                                 | Tushen corpus na gida wanda ke kiyaye ma'aunin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya a lokaci ɗaya (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: a iyaka an fitar da fihirisar tushen da aka yi amfani da shi kwanan nan kuma a sake gina shi akan tambayarsa ta gaba. Manne zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`; ƙimar da ba ta ƙididdigewa ba ta faɗi baya ga tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`                         | `50`                                                                                                | Matsakaicin abubuwan da suka faru na rafi na baya-bayan nan a cikin Dashboard live view buffer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_                                                                                   | Tsawon yanayin mahallin duniya (alamu) ga kowane mai bayarwa wanda ba shi da m `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Lokacin saita, shi** maye gurbin** catalog, dashboard override da ƙimar rajista na waɗannan masu samarwa; lokacin da ba a saita shi, OmniRoute yana amfani da sarkar da aka gina ta, wanda maƙasudinsa na ƙarshe shine `128000`. Dubi Tsawon [mahallin mahallin Mai-mai bayarwa](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`                         | `100`                                                                                               | An tanada karin dakin kan layi yayin bin ka'idojin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Tsawon mahallin mahallin kowane mai bayarwa (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) kuma yana karanta a** kowane mai bayarwa** fom wanda sunansa aka gina daga mai bada ID a lokacin aiki, don haka ba za a iya jera shi a jere a jere ba a cikin tebur na sama ko a `.env.example`.

**Suna.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, inda `<PROVIDER>` shine mai bada ID babba, tare da kowane hali waje `A-Z` / `0-9` maye gurbin `_`. Mai bada ID shine na canonical bayan ƙudurin laƙabi (ɓangaren `provider` na zaren `provider/model` sau ɗaya an warware gajeriyar laƙabi). Buɗe AI- ko nodes masu jituwa na Anthropic suna amfani da kumburin ID da aka samar, don haka UUID wani ɓangare ne na sunan mai canzawa. Ƙimar ita ce ingantacciyar lamba ta alamomi; wani abu (marai, `0`, korau, mara adadi) an yi watsi da shi kuma ƙuduri ya matsa zuwa mataki na gaba.

| Mai bayarwa ID                                              | Sunan mai canzawa                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| wakili na'ura mai ba da hanya tsakanin hanyoyin sadarwa     | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| mai budewa                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Gabatarwa.** Don nau'in `provider/model`, mataki na farko da ke samar da ƙima yana samun nasara:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` ga mai bayarwa.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (yanayin duniya ya wuce).
3. Tagar kowane samfuri: mahallin da aka dage yana juyewa (wanda aka saita a cikin dashboard, ko mai daidaitawar ganowa, duba `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), in ba haka ba ƙasidar / model.dev da aka daidaita.
4. rajistar mai bayarwa `defaultContextLength`.
5. Samfurin-sunan heuristics (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Gina-in na kowane mai bayarwa, sannan `128000`.

Duk matakan yanayi biyu sun shafi** duka mai bayarwa** : kowane samfurin wannan mai bada yana samun iyaka iri ɗaya, kuma sun yi nasara akan abin da ya wuce kowane samfurin dashboard. Lokacin da samfuri ɗaya kawai na mai badawa yana buƙatar taga daban, yi amfani da jujjuya kowane ƙira a cikin dashboard maimakon.

**Iyakar** Wannan sarkar tana tafiyar da duba mahallin mahallin-taga lokacin buƙatu (ƙirar `context_length_exceeded` / "Input ya wuce mahallin mahallin" ƙi a cikin `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), kasafin matsawa da sauri da iyakokin mahallin mahallin. `context_length` da `GET /v1/models` ke tallata har yanzu yana iya fitowa daga kasida ko metadata na rajista kuma ba shi da tabbacin yin nunin ƙetare muhalli.

**Misali.** Mai bada wanda samfurinsa ya karɓi taga alama 1,050,000 yayin da kasidar ta bayar da rahoton 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Sake kunna uwar garken bayan canza shi: ana karanta darajar daga yanayin tsarin uwar garken. A kan** Desktop app** uwar garken yana gudanar da aikin yaro kuma baya ganin tsarin tsarin da aka saita bayan ƙaddamar da app; sanya layin a cikin fayil ɗin `.env` aikace-aikacen yana lodi (tsarin neman a [Electron Jagora: Duba fayil ɗin Muhalli](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup) ), sannan gaba ɗaya barin kuma sake buɗe app ɗin.

### Matsi

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar          | Bayani                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | rashin daidaitawa | Amintaccen aikin `.rtk/filters.json` ba tare da zanta na `.rtk/trust.json` ba. Yi amfani kawai a cikin ci gaban gida mai sarrafawa. |

### Injin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya (shiri na 21)

### Farashin madauki na taron ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya, ƙwarewa, da wartsakewar alama (#10349)

OmniRoute a** Tsarin Node guda ɗaya** . Ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya extraction/retrieval, gwaninta allura, da kuma samar da alamar wartsake suna gudana akan hakan** madauki guda taron** kamar yadda `GET /healthz` da dashboard. Ba zaren ma'aikaci ba ne.

| Aiki                         | Lambar                             | Tsohuwar                                   | Ikon sarrafawa                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Cire ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya/dawowa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dashboard**memoryEnabled** (default a kan) | Kashe** Saituna → Memory** . Babu keɓantaccen maɓalli na kashe env da ya wuce kashe fasalin a cikin saitunan.             |
| alluran gwaninta             | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dashboard**skillsEnabled** (default a kan) | Kashe** Saituna → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Sandbox knobs a ƙasa kawai daure kisa bayan an riga an kunna allura. |
| Alamar wartsakewa            | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Kunna don masu samar OAuth/web masu alaƙa  | Cire haɗin mai badawa ko bari alamun su kasance masu inganci; babu `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` env yau.                             |

Idan `/healthz` yana jinkirin akan akwatin shiru, kashe ƙwarewar ƙwaƙwalwar + da farko, sannan a duba nauyin catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Waɗannan fasalulluka suna haifar da maki `await` amma har yanzu suna gasa don zaren ɗaya.

Haɗa Layer, ma'ajiyar vector da ƙwanƙwasawa don tsarin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya na dindindin (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Mai canzawa                          | Tsohuwar                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (5 min)            | TTL don cache mai shigar da ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya (kowace sa hannun source/model/dim ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | An adana mafi yawan shigarwar LRU a cikin ma'ajiyar sakawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id don fita-in `@huggingface/transformers` bututun MiniLM na gida (~ 23 MB int8, ~ 400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id don madaidaicin tebur potion/Model2Vec mai sakawa. Zazzagewar cikin kasala cikin ma'ajiyar dir.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Littafin da aka yi amfani da shi don adana fayilolin samfurin potion a tsaye. Matsaloli a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR` lokacin da ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Shake tushen Hugging Face Hub tushen URL da `staticPotion.ts` ke amfani dashi (misali madubi na ƙarshen saitin saitin iska).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Tsohuwar top-K da `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector search in `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Matsakaicin Matsayi Fusion akai-akai na `k` ga matasan FTS5 + vector maidowa (Recipe-sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Test/diagnostic seam a cikin `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): lokacin da `true`, tilasta ma'ajiyar vector zuwa `null` (yana kwaikwayi yanayin cloud/WASM ba tare da `sqlite-vec`), mai wulakanta ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya zuwa binciken kalmomin FTS5. Bar rashin saitawa a samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(Ba a saita ba)_           | maɓallin API don Notion backend (wanda aka sani `genericBackend.ts` sanannen saiti na baya yana amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Tushen URL don Notion API (zai iya ƙetare don madadin Notion mai ɗaukar nauyin kansa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(Ba a saita ba)_           | maɓallin API don Obsidian Vault backend (wanda `genericBackend.ts` sanannen saiti na baya ke amfani dashi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Tushen URL don Obsidian Vault API (zai iya ƙetare don vault mai nisa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | TV6 buga memori decay master switch.** Shiga (an kashe tsoho)** — ta share** yana sharewa** ruɓaɓɓen tunanin. Tare da shi, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` suna da tsaftataccen telemetry kuma ba a taɓa share komai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (kwanaki) bayan haka ƙwaƙwalwar `episodic` da ba a yi amfani da ita ba ta lalace. `0` yana sa rigakafi na episodic shima. Nau'u masu ɗorewa (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) koyaushe suna da rigakafi. Agogon lalacewa ya sake kafawa akan `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ƙwaƙwalwar ƙwaƙwalwar da aka yi wa `>=` wannan sau da yawa yakan zama rigakafi ga ruɓe ba tare da la'akari da iri ba. `0` yana hana samun damar rigakafi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (nakasassu)             | Tazara (dakika) don ɓarna na zaɓi na lokaci-lokaci a cikin `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ unset = babu share lokaci-lokaci. Shiga shiga sau biyu: kuma yana buƙatar `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(Ba a saita ba)_           | ID na mai bada waƙafi (wanda ba shi da hankali) wanda ke karɓar saƙon `system`** kawai a index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Don waɗannan, ɓangarorin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya na tsakiyar tsararru ba shi da aminci a cikin tattaunawa mai juyi da yawa, don haka ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya shine merged/prepended azaman jagorar saƙon tsarin maimakon. Tsoffin zuwa `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` kawai; Tsawaita ga wuraren ƙarewa masu jituwa na OpenAI mai ɗaukar nauyin kai (misali Qwen3.5 +/ 3.6) wanda samfurin taɗi wanda ke aiwatar da ƙuntatawar saƙon-jagora guda ɗaya. |

### Low-RAM Docker Misali

`128` dashboard-kawai. Wakilan coding akan wannan tudun `FATAL ERROR` yayin dogon `/v1/responses`. Kar a yi amfani da wannan misalin azaman ƙofar Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## Arena ELO Sync

| Mai canzawa                  | Tsohuwar      | Fayil na asali                                                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Fom ɗin prefix da aka yi amfani da shi don ƙirar ƙira a cikin `GET /v1/models`. `dual` yana tallata duka gajeriyar laƙabi da prefix na canonical ga kowane samfuri (daidaituwar baya — kusan ninki biyu kasida); `alias` yana fitar da id ɗaya akan kowane samfuri; `canonical` yana fitar da cikakken prefix na mai bada-id (masu samar da wanda aka lakafta su shine id na canonical suna kiyaye shigarsu guda). ## PromptQL Mai ba da filin wasa (Unofficial/Experimental)

Gadar zaman zaman GraphQL mai juye-juye don gaggawa.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Duk zaɓin kuskuren — yana nunawa a wuraren ƙarshen filin wasan jama'a; soke kawai don ƙaddamar da self-hosted/alternate PromptQL.

| Mai canzawa                  | Tsohuwar                                                           | Fayil na asali                                                          | Bayani                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Ƙarshen GraphQL da aka yi amfani da shi don ayyukan chat/session.                |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | GraphQL ƙarshen abin da aka yi amfani da shi don tambayar kiredit balance/usage. |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Ƙarshen Ƙarshen da aka yi amfani da shi don mafi kyawun ƙoƙarin-ƙoƙartawa alama. |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Matsakaicin lokacin (ms) don yin `thread_events` kafin lokacin ƙarewa.           |

## Mai Bayar da Yanar HyperAgent (Unofficial/Experimental)

Reverse-engineered session gada don hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). — na zaɓi na zaɓi a wurin ƙarshen billing/usage na jama'a; soke kawai don ƙaddamar da self-hosted/alternate HyperAgent.

| Mai canzawa            | Tsohuwar                                            | Fayil na asali                          | Bayani                                                                           |

| `HYPERAGENT_USAGE_URL` | `https://hyperagent.com/api/settings/billing/usage` | `open-sse/services/usage/hyperagent.ts` | Ƙarshen Ƙarshen da aka yi amfani da shi don ɗauko tubalan kiredit billing/usage. |

## Manufar Budget Amfani mara farashi

| Mai canzawa                    | Tsohuwar                                                        | Fayil na asali                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `UNPRICED_USAGE_BUDGET_POLICY` | _(Ba a saita; kasa rufe sai dai idan an kunna ficewar boolean)_ | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts` | Iyakar maɓalli daily/weekly USD : `fail_closed` yana toshe amfani mara tsada na gaske; `count_as_zero` yana ƙidaya shi a matsayin $0 yayin aiwatar da kashe kuɗi. Ƙididdigar ƙididdiga (DB kafin env) ta soke ficewar boolean. Ƙimar mara inganci da gazawar neman farashi sun gaza rufewa. |
| `USAGE_LIMIT_IGNORE_UNPRICED`  | `false`                                                         | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts` | Fitar da karfin karfin boolean na sama: yana ƙidayar amfani da gaske mara farashi kamar $0 kawai lokacin da ba a daidaita ƙayyadadden ƙayyadaddun ƙayyadaddun ƙididdiga ba. Ana ci gaba da aiwatar da kashe-kashen farashi da gazawar bincike.                                              |

## Ƙididdigar Amfani da Code Kilo

Ma'auni USD na sirri da neman amfani da Kilo Pass don mai bada lambar Kilo. — zaɓi na zaɓi a wuraren jama'a Kilo API; ƙetare kawai don daidaitawar relay/test. Tabbatarwa yana amfani da alamar samun damar OAuth na haɗin haɗin.

| Mai canzawa    | Tsohuwar              | Fayil na asali                        | Bayani                                                                                                |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Tushen URL da aka yi amfani da shi don ɗaukar ma'auni na lambar Kilo na sirri da amfani da Kilo Pass. |

---

## Adobe Firefly Web Provider (Unofficial/Experimental)

Wartsakar da zaman mai bincike don mai ba da gidan yanar gizon Adobe Firefly (`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). — na zaɓin duk abubuwan da suka dace an daidaita su don shigar da tebur na yau da kullun.

> **An cire a #9255.** An maye gurbin tsohon CDP da aka haɗe Chrome Runtime (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) da sabis na shiga na Playwright, kuma kullinsa ba ya wanzu. ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART masu canji, da ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS da ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ba a karanta su a cikin codebase — saitin su ba shi da wani tasiri.

| Mai canzawa                          | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don Chrome mara kai na gaskiya (wanda aka sani-karye don ƙirƙira; cire kuskure kawai).                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | opt-in/out. `0` mai ɗorewa mai bincike yana hana ɗumi mai ɗorewa (murmurewa tsakiyar-tsakiyar 408 har yanzu tana aiki). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Saita zuwa `0` don kashe ci gaba da zaman Adobe Firefly zuwa faifai.                                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Matsakaicin tazarar (ms) da aka aiwatar tsakanin ƙaddamarwa na gaba, ta tsallake tsohowar da aka gina a ciki.           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ƙarin tazari (ms) da aka ƙara bayan tsari mai nasara, wanda ya wuce tsohuwar ginanniyar ciki.                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Jinkirin tushe (ms) kafin ƙaddamar da buƙatun tsara, ƙetare tsohowar da aka gina a ciki.                                |

## 19. Model Sync (Dev)

| Mai canzawa                         | Tsohuwar          | Fayil na asali                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(Ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ƙarfin jujjuyawar samfura.dev daidaita farashin. Cire saitunan martabar = > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no`** nasara akan DB** kuma ya tsallake duka biyun lokaci-lokaci sync da `getModelsDevPricing()` SQL/JSON scans (farfadowa lokacin da dashboard ɗin ke kwance akan madauki ɗaya). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` sojojin daidaita kan. Farashin save/clear har yanzu yana kiran `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, wanda babu-op a ƙarƙashin maƙarƙashiya na mintuna 60 ko `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tazarar daidaita kasida ta lokacin haɓakawa a cikin daƙiƙa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Tazarar (dakika) don gyara mahallin mahallin-taga mai daidaitawa (5004): fitattun windows-mai ba da sanarwar windows daga gano `/models` kamar yadda `auto:discovery` ke tsallakewa lokacin da suka bambanta daga kasidar. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. Yana sake yin amfani da bayanan da aka riga aka daidaita (ba sabon ɗauko); baya sake rubutawa `manual` ya soke.                                                                                                                                                |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` don relays na ɓangare na uku ne kawai waɗanda ke karɓar abokan ciniki na Code Claude keɓaɓɓen. OmniRoute yana sake rubuta buƙatun don haka waɗancan relays ɗin su karɓi su. Idan kawai kuna son amfani da lambar Claude CLI, ko kuma ba ku da tabbacin menene waɗannan relays ɗin, kiyaye wannan naƙasasshe kuma ƙara mai ba da sabis na Anthropic na yau da kullun maimakon.

BudeAI-koguna masu jituwa kawai; bai taɓa yin wuta tare da kiran kayan aiki a cikin jirgin ba.** Lokacin kunnawa:** dogayen tsararraki waɗanda za a yanke tsakiyar amsa kuma kuna karɓar wutsiya da aka kwato ta iso kamar yadda ta fashe maimakon alamar-by-token. Bambance da rashin aiki da ƙayyadaddun lokacin ƙarewa. ### Matsakaicin sake dawo da rafi

Halin riƙewar dawo da shi ana daidaita shi ta madaidaitan madaidaicin a cikin `open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), wanda aka nuna anan don tunani — canza su yana buƙatar gyara lamba, ba env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — tsawon lokacin buɗe SSE taga ana iya sake gwadawa da wuri kafin kowane byte ya sadaukar ga abokin ciniki.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — hula mai wuya akan taga da aka riƙe; aikata (flush + passthrough) da zaran wannan da yawa bytes sun taru, ba tare da la'akari da mai ƙidayar lokaci ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — max a bayyane yake sake buɗe rafi na sama yayin da riƙon baya har yanzu ba a cika aiki ba.

> **Iyakar ƙimar tagar kowane mai bayarwa (babu env var):** FCC-ported ga kowane mai ba da sliding-window-limit _fallback_ yana wanzuwa a lambar (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, waya ta `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma jiragen ruwa tare da** fanko tsoho taswira** kuma yana da** babu mai aiki env var** yau — ana kunna shi ta hanyar ƙugiya / gyara lambar kawai. Ba a jera shi da gangan ba a cikin teburin da ke sama. Matsakaicin gudun ba da sanda na `(token, IP)` wanda _yi_ yana da ƙulli shine `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

---

## 22. Debugging

> [!CAUTION]
> Waɗannan masu canji suna samarwa** fitowar magana** kuma yana iya zubar da mahimman bayanai.** Kar a taɓa kunna samarwa.**

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar                           | Fayil na asali                                                                                        | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(Ba a saita ba)_                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Saita `1` don kunna rajistan ayyukan zartarwar Cursor na verbose (wanda aka yanke chunks SSE, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(Ba a saita ba)_                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Laƙabin `CURSOR_DEBUG` mai jituwa da baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

## Ci gaba da kunna sabis a tsarin kyauta na Render

Render yana sa sabis ɗin yanar gizo na kyauta ya shiga yanayin barci bayan mintuna 15 ba tare da zirga-zirgar shigowa ba ([iyakokin sabis na kyauta](https://render.com/docs/free)). Mai ƙaddamarwa mai zaman kansa (`scripts/dev/run-standalone.mjs`, wanda hoton Docker yake amfani da shi) na iya aika buƙatu lokaci-lokaci zuwa URL ɗin jama’a na sabis. Wannan aikin yana kasancewa a kashe idan babu URL. Ana rubuta kunna shi a rajista, sannan ana ba da rahoton canje-canje kawai a sakamakon buƙatu. Buƙatun ba sa bin umarnin tura su zuwa wani adireshi.

| Ma’auni                      | Ƙimar asali               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SELF_PING_ENABLED`          | A kunne idan an saita URL | `0`, `false`, `off` ko `no` yana kashe mai aika buƙatun. Kashe shi a tsare-tsaren biyan kuɗi idan ba a buƙatar buƙatu lokaci-lokaci.                                                                                                           |
| `SELF_PING_URL`              | `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL`     | URL na tushe na jama’a. Render yana samar da `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL` ga sabis na yanar gizo da shafukan da ba sa canzawa; duba [jagorar ma’aunan muhalli](https://render.com/docs/environment-variables).                                        |
| `SELF_PING_PATH`             | `/api/monitoring/health`  | Dole ya fara da `/` kuma ya warware zuwa adireshi mai asali ɗaya da URL na tushe. URL ko hanya marar inganci yana kashe mai aika buƙatun tare da gargaɗi.                                                                                      |
| `SELF_PING_INTERVAL_SECONDS` | `180`                     | Tazarar buƙatu a cikin daƙiƙu; buƙata ta farko tana gudana bayan daƙiƙu 60. Ƙimar da aka karanta dole ta kasance daga `30` zuwa `2147483`, tare da haɗa iyakokin biyu; ana amfani da `180` idan ƙimar ba ta da inganci ko ta fita daga zangon. |

Sabis da yake aiki a duk wata mai kwanaki 31 yana cin sa’o’in instance 744. Kwatanta wannan amfani da rabon kyauta na Render na yanzu, wanda ake rabawa a duk sararin aiki.

## Yanayin Aiwatarwa

Don jagorar SRE na backend na relay (halayen ts/bifrost/auto, sanya 9router da CLIProxyAPI, da dabarar fallback mai yawan zirga-zirga), duba [Dabarun Backend na Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Ƙaramin Ci gaban A Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Samarwa ta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Ba tare da Haɗin Sadarwa / CI ba

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS tare da Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Sandbox Skills (v3.8.0 +)

Iyakoki da ƙwanƙolin aminci ana amfani da su lokacin da tsarin fasaha (`src/lib/skills/`) yana aiwatar da ƙayyadaddun na'urori masu sarrafa kansa a cikin mahalli mai yashi.

| Mai canzawa                            | Tsohuwar                                | Fayil na asali                                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `10000` (10 s)                          | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ƙayyadaddun lokaci na bangon-agogon aiki don lambar fasaha mai yashi. Ƙarfin wuya; wani abu ya fi tsayi a kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`          | _(ya koma `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwararren Ƙwaƙwalwa Saita sama da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da izinin tafiyar matakai masu yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`                | `1048576` (1 MB)                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da fasaha za ta iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya mai akwatin yashi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`       | `256000` (250 KB)                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da aka ɗauka daga kowane amsa HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin fasaha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS`      | `100000`                                | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ƙarfin wuya a kan haruffan stdout/stderr sun dawo daga kiran akwatin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`       | `false`                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar hanyar sadarwa mai fita daga cikin akwatin yashi. Matsalolin zuwa **ware** domin aminci. Lokacin aikin `agent-sandbox` ya yi watsi da shi: keɓantawar hanyar sadarwa, tsarin fayil ɗin karantawa kawai da iyakokin CPU/memory sun fito daga `SandboxTemplate` na afareta.                                                                                                                                 |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`        | _(Ba komai)_                            | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin izinin waƙafi-wakafi na hotunan kwantena da aka halatta don aiwatar da akwatin yashi. Babu komai yana nufin ginannen tsoho kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`          | _(wanda aka gina a ciki)_               | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Hoton kwantena da aka yi amfani da shi lokacin zubar da akwatin yashi mai goyon bayan Docker. Juye don saka hoton tushe na al'ada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`               | `auto`                                  | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Lokacin aikin kwantena don gwaninta sandboxing: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman` \| `agent-sandbox`. `auto` yana zaɓar mafi kyawun lokacin da aka shigar kowane mai masaukin OS (Apple Container/OrbStack akan macOS, WSL Container akan Windows, Podman akan Linux), komawa zuwa Docker. `agent-sandbox` (kubernetes dumin wuraren waha mai nisa) ficewa ne kawai kuma ba a taɓa gano shi ta atomatik ba. |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_POOLS`           | _(Ba komai)_                            | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | `image=pool` nau'i-nau'i, waƙafi-rabu: wanda `SandboxWarmPool` ke hidima ga kowane hoton sandbox lokacin `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME=agent-sandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_POOL`            | _(Ba komai)_                            | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Tafkin dumi na faɗuwa don hotunan da ba a jera su a cikin `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_POOLS` ba. Gudun ya gaza (babu koma baya ga kwantena na gida) lokacin da ba a warware ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_KUBECONFIG`      | _(kubectl tsoho)_                       | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Kubeconfig don wakili-sandbox runtime. Yana buƙatar kawai da'awar akwatin sandbox create/get/list/watch/delete, kwas ɗin samun, pods/exec ƙirƙira a cikin sarari suna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_KUBECTL`         | `kubectl`                               | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | kubectl binary da wakili-sandbox ke amfani da lokacin gudu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_CONTAINER`       | `main`                                  | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Sunan kwantena a cikin akwatin yashi wanda ake aiwatar da umarni a ciki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_AGENT_SANDBOX_CLAIM_TIMEOUT_S` | `60`                                    | `src/lib/skills/agentSandboxProvider.ts`                           | Daƙiƙa don jira da'awar zama Shirye (max 600). Ana share da'awar idan ba ta taɓa yin hakan ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ƙaddamar da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fita daga lambar fasaha ta sabani. Haɗa tare da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da madaidaicin `CORS_ORIGIN`/manufofin wakili a cikin abubuwan da aka raba.

## 25. Ƙayyadaddun Masu Samarwa, Tunnels, Ajiyayyen Bayanai da Sauran Lokutan Gudanarwa

Soke fallback na alaƙar zaman Redis (ana kunna shi ne kawai idan an zaɓa; tsohon tsarin SQLite bai canza ba):

Kowane ma’ajiyar alaƙar Redis yana karɓar aƙalla ayyuka 8 da ba su kammala ba; idan ya kai iyakar, kira yana amfani da SQLite nan take, kuma guraben suna ci gaba da kasancewa a cike har sai aikin Redis na asali ya daidaita, ko da bayan wa’adin mai kira na 100 ms ya wuce.

- R1. Duk wata amsar Redis da aka kawo cikin lokacin aikin zaman ita ce tushen gaskiya: canje-canjen da aka karɓa, CAS da aka ƙi/ɓace, maɓallan da suka ɓace, gogewar maɓallan da ba su wanzu, da kuma nasarori ko rashin nasarar cirewa daga ajiya suna share layin fallback na gida na wannan zaman kai tsaye, ba tare da `await` tsakanin amsa da sharewa ba. Sharewar tana da aminci (a mafi muni, za a sake zaɓe sau ɗaya yayin wata katsewar aiki ta gaba); layin da ya tsufa ba shi da aminci.
- R2. Aikin da ya ƙare wa’adinsa ko ya samu kuskure kuma ya rubuta fallback na gida yana adana ainihin abin da ya rubuta a matsayin bajiti da aka tsara. Idan umarnin Redis ɗinsa ya kammala daga baya, SQLite compare-and-delete na aiki tare yana share wannan layin ne kawai idan bajitin ya yi daidai ƙwarai, ba ta hanyar daidaita asusu ko haɗin kai ba. Sabon rubutun da aka yi yayin katsewar aiki yana nan daram.
- R3. Cirewa daga ajiya koyaushe yana cire pin na gida na asusun da ya gaza, har ma a lokacin katsewar aiki; sarrafa amsarsa daga nan yana bin R1/R2. Redis mai lafiya ba ya taɓa rubuta pin na gida: babu kwafin madubi ko kundin tsara jeri, kuma samfurori na iya bambanta a lokacin katsewar aiki.

Ƙarshen quota na masu samarwa, tunnels na cibiyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), tafkin fitar da bayanai na 1Proxy, ajiyayyen bayanan rumbun adana bayanai, da ƙananan sauye-sauye na kowane fasali da aka ambata a layin executor ko cikin scripts.

| Canjin yanayi                                  | Tsoho                                                                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SESSION_AFFINITY_STORE`             | `sqlite`                                                                    | `src/lib/db/sessionAffinity/storeFactory.ts`                              | Manhajar baya ta daidaita zaman da asusu. `redis` tare da saita `REDIS_URL` ne kawai ke kunna rabawa; ƙimomin da ba a saita ba ko waɗanda ba a sani ba, ko rashin tsarin Redis, suna amfani da SQLite. Yana buƙatar `sessionAffinityTtlMs` mai ƙima mai kyau. Hanyar Redis tana adana bayanan asalin asusun amfani da aka yi hash tare da ID ɗin haɗi; hanyar SQLite ta tsoho tana riƙe da tsarin JSON mai filaye huɗu na gado. Tana daidaita asali a cikin tafkin gida da ya cancanta kafin ID. Tana amfani da `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX` na abokin ciniki da aka raba, ƙimomin JSON da TTL na `PX`; canje-canje suna kwatanta ƙimar da aka lura da ita ainihin. Amsoshin Redis masu lafiya suna soke madadin katsewar gida, kuma amsa da ta makara za ta iya share takamaiman layin madadin da aka jera shi daidai, wanda wannan aikin da lokaci ya ƙare ya rubuta. Ana sake tabbatar da fil da aka samo tun da wuri lokacin amfani. Kurakuran Redis, ƙarewar lokaci ko cunkoson karɓa suna komawa SQLite tare da gargaɗi masu iyakancewa (iyakar 100 ms a kowane kiran ajiya); rashin samun sakamako daga Redis cikin nasara ba ya karanta shadow. Ba ya raba dagewar haɗin combo, fil ɗin zagayen Codex na asali, cunkoson asusu, kullewa ko iyakokin kashe kuɗi. |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Kirtanin haɗin Redis don manhajar baya ta mai iyakance ƙimar amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Bayani ne mai matuƙar muhimmanci kuma mai alaƙa da zaman; an fi so a yi amfani da filin Dashboard na haɗin kai `qwenCloudCookie`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
