Idan an saita ta, buƙatun daga IP da ba na loopback ba dole ne su ɗauki `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. Ana buƙatarta lokacin da `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` a tura-kayan da ba na loopback ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_CONTAINER_NAME`               | `omniroute-redis`                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Sunan kwantena na mai ƙaddamar da Redis da dannawa sau ɗaya (`omniroute redis up`). CLI da GUI na `RedisLauncherPanel` duka suna amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_HOST_PORT`                    | `6379`                                                                      | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Tashar host don mai ƙaddamar da Redis da dannawa sau ɗaya. Ƙara ƙimar idan host ɗin ya riga ya ɗaure 6379. Tashar cikin kwantenar tana nan 6379.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST`                    | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Fuskar sadarwar host da mai ƙaddamar da Redis da dannawa sau ɗaya ke wallafa a kanta. Mai ƙaddamarwa yana fara Redis BA TARE da kalmar sirri ba, saboda haka ɗaurewa da `0.0.0.0` zai bai wa kowane host da ke kan LAN ɗinka damar samun Redis marar tantancewa — faɗaɗa wannan ne kawai idan ka sa kalmar sirri a kan instans ɗin da kanka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Fuskar sadarwar host da docker-compose ke wallafa Redis sidecar a kanta (#9286). Compose Redis yana aiki ba tare da `requirepass` ba; kwantenan app suna isa gare shi ta hanyar sadarwar compose (`redis:6379`) — tashar da aka wallafa tana samuwa ne kawai don kayan aikin da ke gefen host. `0.0.0.0` yana fallasa Redis marar tantancewa ga dukkan LAN.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Tashar host don Redis sidecar na compose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `APP_BIND_HOST`                                | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-compose.prod.yml`                           | Fuskar sadarwar host da docker-compose ke wallafa dashboard/API/live-WS na app ɗin a kanta (#12568). Tare da `REQUIRE_API_KEY=false` da ake samarwa a matsayin tsohuwar ƙimar `.env.example`, `0.0.0.0` yana fallasa wakilin LLM na `/v1` marar tantancewa ga dukkan LAN/WAN — faɗaɗa wannan ne kawai bayan an sa `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ko wani reverse proxy a gabansa ya tilasta nasa tantancewar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QDRANT_BIND_HOST`                             | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Fuskar sadarwar host da docker-compose ke wallafa Qdrant memory sidecar a kanta (#12578). Dalilin kariya daga fallasar LAN iri ɗaya ne da na `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `BIFROST_BIND_HOST`                            | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Fuskar sadarwar host da docker-compose ke wallafa Bifrost router sidecar a kanta (#12578). Dalilin kariya daga fallasar LAN iri ɗaya ne da na `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX`                             | `omniroute:`                                                                | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Fiffiken sararin suna da ake amfani da shi ga kowane maɓallin OmniRoute Redis (mai iyakance ƙima, ma’ajin tantancewa, ma’ajin kaso, mai katse da’irar dumama). Yana hana karo-karon maɓallai lokacin da ake raba instans ɗin Redis da wasu manhajoji (#11042).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Mai Tsara Claude Warmup

Warmup da cron ke tafiyarwa don haɗin Anthropic OAuth da aka zaɓa, ta yadda buɗe tagar iyakar amfani ta sa’o’i 5 zai faru ne ta hanyar buƙatar tsarawa mai sauƙi maimakon buƙatar farko ta gaske (#8848). Mai tsara yana kashe a tsohuwa sai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` yana da ƙimar truthy **kuma** an yi alamar haɗin a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; jerin haɗi maras komai yana nufin ba za a yi wa komai warmup ba ko da an kunna canjin muhalli.

| Variable                       | Default                     | Source File                  | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(unset → off)_             | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babbar maɓallin kunna mai tsara warmup. Yana karɓar `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ba tare da la’akari da manyan ko ƙananan haruffa ba, kuma bayan cire sarari). Duk wata ƙima dabam, ko rashin saita ta, yana barin mai tsara a kashe. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Bayanin cron mai filaye biyar don lokacin warmup, wanda ake kimantawa a `America/Los_Angeles` (yankin lokacin sake saita Anthropic) ba tare da la’akari da agogon na’ura mai ɗauke da sabar ba.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Yawan haɗin da ake yi wa warmup a layi ɗaya a kowane lokaci. Ana takaita shi zuwa `1`-`10`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa `3`.                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Samfurin da ake amfani da shi don buƙatar warmup. A sauya shi ne kawai idan samfurin na tsohuwa bai samuwa a tsarin ku ba; a zaɓi samfurin mafi arha da har yanzu ke buɗe tagar.                                                |

### Zaman VNC na Shiga Ta Mai Bincike da Laƙabin Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC da aka saka cikin akwati, wanda ake amfani da shi wajen karɓar bayanan shaidar shiga ta mai bincike cikin hulɗa (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da tsohon laƙabin `DATA_DIR`. Dukansu zaɓi ne — saitunan VNC na tsohuwa suna nuni zuwa hoton `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗa, kuma ana sauya su ne kawai don hoton akwati na musamman, tashoshi, ko daidaita tsarin rayuwar akwati.

| Variable                              | Default                                                                  | Source File                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alamar hoton Docker na kwandon shiga na Chromium+VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko nuna wannan zuwa hoton al'ada.                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Binari na runtime na kwantena da ake amfani da shi wajen ƙaddamar da kwandon VNC (misali, saita shi zuwa `podman`).                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC/noVNC da aka fallasa a cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar kundin bayanan martabar Chromium a cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin mai masaukin da ke riƙe da bayanan martabar shiga burauza da aka adana.                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (10 min)                                                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewa na rashin aiki (ms) kafin a share zaman VNC da ba ya aiki.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (30 min)                                                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar tsawon rayuwa (ms) na zaman VNC guda ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana lokaci guda.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) na jiran burauzar da ke cikin kwandon ta kasance a shirye don CDP.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) na tattara zaman/cookies da aka kama bayan an kammala shiga.                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni da ake miƙawa ga Chromium da ke cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwandon shiga na VNC ke shiga (#12571) maimakon gada ta tsohuwa, don haka kwantena ’yan’uwa ba za su iya isa tashar gadar CDP ɗinsa ba.                                              |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(unset)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Sunan laƙabi na gado** na `DATA_DIR`, ana duba shi ne kawai bayan `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` duk ba a saita su ba. Yana gano ma'ajiyar zaman zaren yanar gizo na Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Tsarin Gwaji & E2E

Ana amfani da su ta `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Bar kowace
ƙima da ke ƙasa ba a saita ba a cikin tura samarwa (production).

| Mai Canji                              | Tsoho                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin bootstrap na E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) ga mai gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | yana komawa zuwa `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kalmar sirri ta admin da aka shigar a cikin muhallin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe binciken lafiya na gida yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madaukar binciken lafiya na token OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka fitar daga zagayowar sabunta token na gaggawa (misali `codex,openai`). Madadin da aka yi niyya ga kashe binciken lafiya gaba ɗaya — masu samarwa na gajeren TTL suna ci gaba da sabuntawa yayin da masu samarwa na cascade ke kasancewa masu amsa kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe hayaniyar binciken lafiya a cikin Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin production na Next.js kafin Playwright ya fara (kyautata CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                 | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake hook na cire OmniRoute (CI yana amfani da shi don riƙe `node_modules` cikakke).                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                            | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Lokacin jira (ms) don uwar garke ta sami lafiya kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`      | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL da tsarin smoke na Electron yake sa ran cewa aikace-aikacen da aka kunna zai bauta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimlar ƙarar lokaci (ms) kafin tsarin smoke ya daina.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tagar daidaitawa (ms) bayan shafin ya lodi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(atomatik)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Hanyar da aka bayyana zuwa ga executable na Electron da aka kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jagorar bayanai don gudanar da smoke na Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita `1` domin kiyaye kundayen bayanan gwajin smoke bayan gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita `1` domin yawo log na Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake ƙaddamarwa a kan wannan kundayen bayanan kuma tabbatar cewa ƙaddamarwa ta biyu tana zaɓar direban SQLite na gargajiya.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(binciken PATH)_                   | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Sake rubuta hanyar binary na Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Layin sarrafa fassarar takardun

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run`).
Ba a saita dukkan masu canji shida ba tare da saita su ba — saita su a cikin `.env` kawai
a kan na'urorin da ya kamata su iya gudanar da mai fassara takardun.

| Mai canji                                  | Na asali                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | URL na tushe mai jituwa da OpenAI don backend na fassarawa.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Alamar Bearer don backend na fassarawa (ba a taɓa rajista shi ba).                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | ID na samfuri, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Sarkar samfuran da aka raba da waƙafi na zaɓi wanda ake gwadawa bisa tsari lokacin da samfurin farko ya gaza (429 quota, 401/402, 5xx, timeout, network); ba a sake gwada buƙata marar kyau (400/413/422) a wani wuri ba.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`                 | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ƙaren lokaci na kowace buƙata a millisecond.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Kulawar tunani na zaɓi, ana aika shi yadda yake zuwa backend idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4` (LLM), `8` (Google) | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ayyukan fassarar fayil/yanki a layi ɗaya. `--concurrency` yana maye gurbin saitin mahalli; ana sarrafa yawan buƙatun Google masu gudana a lokaci guda daban a ƙasa.                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`                   | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | `llm` yana amfani da backend na LLM da aka saita. `google` yana zaɓar Google a matsayin injin farko kuma baya buƙatar sauye-sauyen backend na LLM.                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`                     | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ana kunna shi ta tsohuwa tare da injin LLM: idan fassarar sashe ta gaza bayan sake gwajin tabbatarwa ko kuskuren samfur, ana gwada Google a matsayin madadin kuma ana tabbatar da fitowarsa. Saita `0` don kashe shi.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Maɓallin Cloud Translation v2 na zaɓi. Idan ba a saita shi ba, buƙatu suna amfani da endpoint na yanar gizo marar maɓalli.                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`                     | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Cikakkiyar lamba da ta fi sifili da ke iyakance buƙatun Google masu gudana a lokaci guda, wadda yankuna masu amfani da iyakar da aka saita iri ɗaya a cikin tsari guda suke rabawa. Ana ƙin ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba kafin kowace buƙata. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`                 | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata a cikin milisekan, a matsayin cikakkiyar lamba daga `1` zuwa `2147483647`. Ana ƙin ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba kafin kowace buƙata.                                                                             |
| `UI_KIT_READ_TOKEN`                        | _(Ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-upstream.mjs` | Alamar karantawa kawai don mai zaman kansa na sama UI kit repo, wanda `npm run check:ui-kit-upstream` ke amfani da shi da aikin yau da kullun "UI Kit upstream watch". A cikin gida cak ɗin yana amfani da bayanan git ɗin ku.               |

## 27. Ciyarwar Radar (Tallafin Kai)

Ƙarin zaɓi na zaɓi da alamar fasalin RADAR_ENABLED (tsoho daga — alamar alama da aka kunna ta Settings/DB, ba env var ba; duba [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Matsaloli huɗu na farko da ke ƙasa ƙetare na zaɓi na zaɓi don ciyarwa mai ɗaukar nauyi ko cokali mai yatsa da maɓalli mai goyan baya. Na biyar, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, shine keɓan hanyar haɗin kai marar-tsari zuwa rukunin ayyuka masu zaman kansu na mai shi. Dubi [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) don cikakken tsarin doc da [kunnawar ƙarshen zuwa-ƙarshensa da jeri na saitin jagora](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Ba a saita mai karanta shelar Home/Changelog gabaɗaya ta hanyar canjin yanayi kuma baya dogara da alamar fasalin RADAR_ENABLED. Yana karanta ma'ajiyar jama'a `news.json` URL da aka ayyana a cikin `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` ta GET kawai; sallamar IDs ya kasance a cikin ma'ajiyar gida mai lilo.

| Mai canzawa                   | Tsohuwar                                     | Fayil na asali                                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Tushen URL wanda aka raba ta keɓaɓɓen kasidar sa hannu, masu ba da shawara, tayin tallafi, da ciyarwar Intel. Shake don nunawa a sabis mai ɗaukar nauyi ko cokali mai yatsa. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(maɓallin tsoho mai haɗawa)_                | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ed25519 maɓalli na jama'a (base64-DER SPKI ko PEM) ana amfani dashi don tabbatar da sa hannun ciyarwa daga ciyarwar ta al'ada.                                               |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL maɓallin dashboard ɗin "Ni mai ba da gudummawa ne" yana buɗewa (GitHub OAuth maɓallin da'awar maɓalli).                                                                  |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL maɓallin dashboard ɗin "Tallafawa aikin" yana buɗewa ( shafin payment/plans ).                                                                                           |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(Ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Mallakar ayyuka masu zaman kansu kawai-hanyar hanyar haɗin gwiwa. ana buƙatar HTTPS sai dai SSH HTTP gaba; unset ko mara inganci ƙirƙira babu abin kewayawa.                 |

## Audit: Cire / Matattu Canje-canje

Masu canji masu zuwa sun bayyana a sigar baya ta `.env.example` amma suna da** babu runtime references** a cikin codebase na yanzu. An cire su:

| Mai canzawa                                                                                                                                                                     | Dalili                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Kada a taɓa karanta ta kowane fayil na tushe. SQLite shine kawai direba mai goyan baya — babu zaɓi da ake buƙata.                                        |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Gabatar da tsoffin samfuran docs/env amma ba a yi amfani da su ba a lokacin aiki. Maiyuwa zai dawo a cikin sifa mai nau'i-nau'i na gaba.                 |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ba a magana a cikin lambar tushe. Girman ma'ajin bayanai ba shi da iyaka ta hanyar wucin gadi.                                                           |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ba a magana a cikin lambar tushe. An yi yuwuwa an cire gogewar gado.                                                                                     |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ba a rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                                |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ba a rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding yana amfani da OAuth, ba binary CLI ).                                             |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ba a magana a ko'ina a cikin lambar tushe.                                                                                                               |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | An cire a cikin v3.8.0. Lokacin gudu baya karanta waɗannan takaddun env vars — sun fito daga Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / rufaffen DB. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | An cire a cikin v3.8.0. Mai aiwatar da Cursor yana amfani da `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (duba §22).                                          |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire a cikin v3.8.0. Kiro yana cikin `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — jujjuyawar sa ba ta da wani tasiri.                                          |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi tare da sauran maɓallan maɓalli API da ba a amfani da su a cikin v3.8.0.                                                                     |

### Tsohuwar Ƙimar Gyara

| Mai canzawa               | Tsohuwar darajar `.env.example` | Ainihin Code Default | Kafaffen                                           |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                            | `7`                  | ✅ Cire ƙima mai ɓarna; an rubuta `7` azaman tsoho |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                            | `7`                  | ✅ Cire ƙima mai ɓarna; an rubuta `7` azaman tsoho |

### sake fasalin tsarin OpenCode (ad-hoc kayan aiki)

Ana amfani da `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` don sabunta `opencode.json` tare da madaidaitan ma'aunin `limit.context` da `limit.output` waɗanda aka ja daga misalin OmniRoute mai gudana. Babu ɗayan waɗannan da ake buƙata don aiki na yau da kullun — rubutun shine kayan aikin haɓakawa kawai.

| Mai canzawa        | Tsohuwar                 | Fayil na asali                            | Bayani                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Tushen URL na misalin OmniRoute don tambayar `/v1/models`.                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | maɓallin API don tabbatarwa akan ƙarshen `/v1/models` OmniRoute Faɗuwa zuwa `OPENCODE_API_KEY` lokacin da ba a saita ba.    |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(Ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Buɗe-style API key (`sk-...`) rubuta a cikin sabunta `opencode.json`. Faɗuwa zuwa `OMNIROUTE_KEY` lokacin da ba a saita ba. |

### Matsawa layi-eval kayan aiki (ad-hoc kayan aiki)

Ana amfani da `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, ƙimar matsi ta layi CLI. Ba a buƙatar aiki na yau da kullun na kayan aikin haɓaka — kawai.

| Mai canzawa                  | Tsohuwar        | Fayil na asali                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (ba komai) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Takaddun shaidar JSON da aka kawo mai aiki don mai bayarwa wanda aka yi amfani da shi ta hanyar matsawa-eval CLI na layi (wanda aka kwatanta da `JSON.parse`). Bar unset don bushe gudu. |

### VNC Mai Binciken Bincike

Ana amfani da `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` don saita zaman Chromium mara kai na tushen Docker don masu samar da injin bincike. Duk abubuwan da suka dace — na zaɓi wanda aka nuna a ƙasa.

| Mai canzawa                           | Tsohuwar                       | Fayil na asali                              | Bayani                                                                              |

| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar zuwa binary Docker da aka yi amfani da ita don zubar da kwantena VNC.        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | hoton Docker don kwandon Chromium na VNC.                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(tutocin da aka gina a ciki)_ | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin Chromium CLI args ya wuce zuwa mai binciken da ke cikin akwati.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | tashar VNC cikin akwati.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol tashar jiragen ruwa a cikin akwati.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Littafin bayanin martaba a cikin akwati.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Jagorar-gefen mai watsa shiri don bayanan martabar burauza masu tsayi.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kashe lokacin aiki (ms) kafin a girbe zaman VNC.                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin lokacin zama (ms).                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin zaman VNC na lokaci guda.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin shirye-shiryen Browser (ms).                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | lokacin Harvest/cleanup (ms).                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙaddamar da hanyar sadarwar Docker kwandon ya haɗa (#12571), a kashe tsohuwar gada. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(Ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Directory don jimrewar zaman zaren Notion.                                          |

| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adireshin ɗaure don sabis ɗin Redis da aka haɗa.           |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Tashar jiragen ruwa don hidimar Redis da aka haɗa.         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Ƙaddamar da OmniRoute mai iyaka don adreshin ɗauren Redis. |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Saitunan asali na gama-gari don yanayin gudanar da burauza da tunnel ɗin kayan aiki mai fita. Ƙimomin haɗin da aka saita a dashboard suna da fifiko.

| Maɓalli                                 | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium don shigarwar npm, systemd da PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Madadin gama-gari don takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Maƙurar CDP ta ciki; Docker yana amfani da sidecar a tashar `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(ba a saita ba — a kashe)_    | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`                   | Saita zuwa `1` (tare da saita maƙurar CDP) don sake amfani da shafin ChatGPT na yanzu da ke cikin bayanin martabar burauza da ake sarrafawa daga waje kuma aka shiga cikinsa, maimakon keɓantaccen mahalli a kowane zagaye; haka kuma yana iyakance shafukan ChatGPT masu aiki lokaci guda zuwa 1. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa. Duba [bayanan martabar CDP mai ɗorewa](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                                | Sirrin da ake rabawa wanda ake buƙata a cikin header na `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` don kowace buƙatar HTTP ta wakilin CDP da haɓakawa zuwa WebSocket. Idan ba a saita shi ba, wakilin zai loda ko samar da token ta amfani da `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN_FILE`; idan babu token mai aiki, zai ƙi kowace buƙata. Samar da shi da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                        |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | ID na ramin OpenAI na gama-gari don zagayen kayan aikin Codex na gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Maɓallin lokacin gudanarwa na rami na gama-gari; kada a taɓa saka shi cikin rajistan ayyuka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Ainihin sunan sabon mahaɗin musamman na ChatGPT da aka ƙirƙira don gadar MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Kundin adireshi na musamman don yanayin burauza, dillali da rami.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-diagnostics.ts` | Idan an saita zuwa `1`, yana ɗaukar hotunan binciken matsalar burauza a kowane wurin dubawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Cikakkiyar hanya ta zaɓi zuwa binary na mai ƙaddamarwa mai ɗorewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Cikakkiyar hanya ta zaɓi zuwa binary na lokacin gudanarwar Bun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Tsohon madadi na `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; sabbin shigarwa suna amfani da sunan da aka ƙayyade.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

## OmniConductor Bridge

Mabukacin SSE mai tsayi wanda ke nuna ayyukan cibiyar OmniConductor zuwa cikin A2A TaskManager na gida (`src/lib/conductor/`). Shiga — gada tana farawa ne kawai lokacin da aka saita `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Gefen uwar garken kawai: alamar cibiya ba dole ba ne ta isa mai lilo.

| Mai canzawa                    | Tsohuwar                | Fayil na asali                   | Bayani                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(Ba komai)_            | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Tushen URL na cibiyar OmniConductor (misali `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). An kashe gadar =.                                  |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(Ba komai)_            | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Takaddun shaida don ciyarwar — SSE suna fitar da tsaran `spokesperson` -kind akan cibiyar (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).     |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(Ba komai)_            | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Takaddun shaida don inbound A2A→ tawagar ɗawainiya (`POST /v1/tasks`); ya koma `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` idan ba a saita ba. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | Tushen URL na sabis na mai magana da yawun (Faro) a bayan dashboard chat proxy (`/api/conductor/ask`).                   |

### Jadawalin ƙididdiga-sani

Ana amfani da `open-sse/services/combo.ts` da `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` don duba kasafin kuɗi na alamar kafin buƙatun. Fita-in — tsohuwar dabi'ar tuƙi ba ta canzawa lokacin da ba a saita ba.

| Mai canzawa                        | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`      | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Lokacin da `1`, tsallake hanyoyin haɗin gwiwa waɗanda kasafin kuɗin tagar kowane-taga (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tebur `provider_quota_state`) ba za su iya biyan ƙimar da aka ƙiyasta kafin aikawa ba. Rashin buɗewa lokacin da ba a saita kasafin kuɗi ba.                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(na)_   | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Gyaran martani na ƙididdige alamar tsaro na mahallin (#14931): kowane (mai bayarwa, samfuri, kayan aikin da ake bayarwa) EMA na `usage.prompt_tokens` / kimanta, manne zuwa 0.15-2.0, a cikin ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya kawai. `off`, `0` ko `false` yana hana yin rikodi da aikace-aikace. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false`  | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Yi rijistar `xai-oauth` (`xao`) da hanyoyin hoton `grok-cli`. Dashboard alama tuta mai suna iri ɗaya; a soke DB yayi nasara akan wannan darajar.                                                                                                                                   |

## Gwajin aiki tare da samfuri

| Mai canzawa                    | Tsohuwar | Fayil na asali                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_MODEL_SYNC_TIMINGS` | `0`      | `src/lib/providerModels/modelSyncTimings.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don shiga lokacin lokaci, buƙatun da aka samar da ID da sakamakon success/error na lokaci yayin aiki tare da ƙira. An kashe don duk sauran dabi'u. Logs sun watsar da buƙatun, asusu, katalogi da abubuwan biyan kuɗi na kuskure. |

## Kayan aikin nazarin kafofin watsa labarai na OpenCode na cikin gida

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/cli/omniroute-media-mcp.mjs`. Saita waɗannan a cikin yanayin aikin kayan aikin na cikin gida.

| Sauyi                      | Tsoho                                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_CONFIG`   | `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/opencode/opencode.json` (ko `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json`) | Tsarin OpenCode da kayan aikin kafofin watsa labarai da mai sakawa ke karantawa.                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_PROVIDER` | `omniroute`                                                                       | Shigar mai samarwa da kayan aikin kafofin watsa labarai ke karantawa daga tsarin OpenCode.                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_MODEL`    | `auto`                                                                            | Samfurin da kayan aikin kafofin watsa labarai ke aika. Dole ne mai samar da shi ya goyi bayan nau’ikan kafofin watsa labarai da aka nema. |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_API_KEY`  | Maɓallin API na mai samarwa da aka tsara ko ma’anar yanayinsa                     | Shaidar zaɓi don sauyawa. Ajiye shi a cikin yanayin aikin na cikin gida; kada a taɓa ƙaddamar da shi.                                     |
